- **The other half: who pressed the brake, and when.** The historian's four levellers (a great war, a revolution, the
  collapse of a state, a plague); in quiet years the gap "grew or stood still"; the levellers "levelled downwards".
  **The author's thesis of 14 is tied to it** ("fights for justice make the rich poorer and do not make the poor
  richer... He wrote the same with tables, and more carefully than I did"). Oleg: "That is not a brake, that is a
  wall." **Under the list**: the plague (wages about doubled in England; the law of 1351 "did not hold": "You cannot
  give orders to the only man left") and the mass war ("You cannot take a man's son and leave his neighbour's
  millions alone"; the count of twenty countries over two centuries). Oleg: "Hands... When everybody is needed,
  everybody gets something." **"That is the brake under the brakes... The tax is the form. The need for people is
  the force"** (the lock with 47: money is a record that somebody needed you).
- **The machines are named by Oleg, after the middle**: "Stop. I can see where this log is rolling. Those ones. If
  hands are not needed, and heads not much either... — I did not say it. — You sat and waited for me to say it. Then
  the brake has nothing to hold on to."
- **The author's two holes of 2012** (the argument under the SETI talk): the sum of a thousand men, 999 out of work,
  and the lock with 25 ("the levy holds while the voter has in his hands something the technosphere needs"); Oleg's
  "one man, one vote" is answered with the second hole, **"the heap can leave"** (the author's example: ninety per
  cent everywhere, one country cuts to forty-five, the capital of ten runs to it, it collects five times more, the
  nine others lower their rates and benefits). Fresh evidence: the average rate on company profits 40% in 1980 and
  23.5% in 2024; the floor of fifteen agreed in 2021 by about 140 countries. "A man can be taxed where he lives. A
  heap lives where it pays less."
- **Oleg's third brake, death and heirs** ("The merchant earns, the son keeps, the grandson drinks it away"):
  accepted ("I ought to have named it myself"), the lock with 44. It works on a man; a firm does not die of age, it
  is swallowed (40), and "swallowed" in the game is the loser's matches going to the winner: **"the heaps that have
  neither heirs nor funerals play the same game among themselves. Without that brake."**
- **The author's debt of 09 is paid** (a reader: some will profit by the machines and the rest will starve; the
  author answered only "everybody is doomed"): "that was no answer. For the first rounds he was right... But then
  the some sit down with one another at the same table. The game does not end when the poor have dropped out of
  it." Who holds the box at the end: "Not necessarily a man" (the player of 37 that never gives back; "every real
  game leaves something on the table itself"); deferred to "Who owns the tractor" (55).
- **The promise of 43 and 47 is kept, and the answer stays "I don't know"**: among the machines everything
  multiplies (users, learning, chips; "Stakes in shares. And a bent coin"), so "the slope is known: towards one".
  The brakes, counted by Oleg: owners who are different people; several states; copying (the distillation of 45).
  Andrey: "That is a levy in kind: knowledge leaks from the big heap into the small ones, and nobody votes for it.
  Chips do not leak. Which is the stronger I don't know... without a brake, into one. And the first two brakes are
  made of people." Whether one is good: "A separate evening" (62).
- No villain and no genius in the game. The reader's objection under 14 (the beggar and the neighbour with three
  rams) is answered: "About the feeling he was right. It is not the gap that has changed. It is what holds it."
  "— Gloomy. — But objective."
- Strengths stated: solid (the arithmetic, "it is a theorem"; the two games on the computer; the long tails "as
  things measured"; the narrowing between 1914 and the seventies and its return); argued about (how much of a
  fortune is luck; whether three corrections that fit a curve explain it; whether only catastrophes level, "the
  historian is reproached with the quiet decades after the war"; the size of the narrowing and the return);
  Andrey's (the need for people under the brakes; the brake goes with the need; the machines' lines are on the same
  slope; "who holds the box at the end is not a conclusion").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; seventeenth tick "again": "Came with a box of matches. Won the box. Came out at
  those ones, and said it myself." Oleg crosses out the notebook line "Why money goes to money" (written in 34).

**Images and devices used**: the tenth entry in the notebook of dreams (payday at the plant, wages paid in matches
out of one box, one match short; nobody rang; "Ten dreams and not one call. Your firewood is safe so far"); the box
shaken at the ear; three matches into the fire; the five-rouble coin; "The coin knows its master"; the single match
held up; the strokes in the notebook; "Around twenty" crossed out; the two rows in the ash; a thousand Olegs with
crumbs; the man in the middle of the table; "a fifth of nothing"; the hill and the tail; "Nobody gets up a hundred
million times earlier"; the forestry's two prices and bearings by the wagon; the bent fingers (twice); the common
pot; the screw; "not a brake, a wall"; "the only man left"; the log that rolls; the heap that crosses a border in a
second and Oleg "with the dacha, the wife and the language"; the merchant family with half the high street; the
table that keeps something; "Honestly won. — Bring it tomorrow. I have nothing left to light the fire with"; the
teaser ("Who thought these rules up?... — Nobody. It came out that way. — 'It came out.' And it is going to rain
tomorrow. — What is?").

**Facts used**: **mostly from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before
reusing**. Computed for the article: every figure of the two games and of the levy (`tools/yard_sale.py`, seed 49;
the rule of the plain game, in which a player with no matches can still win, is the standard one and is stated in
the text). Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref records of the DOIs linked (Chakraborti 2002, *Int. J. Mod.
Phys. C*; Dragulescu & Yakovenko 2000, *Eur. Phys. J. B*; Boghosian, Johnson & Marcq 2015, *J. Stat. Phys.*;
Pluchino, Biondo & Rapisarda 2018, *Advances in Complex Systems*; Fagereng, Guiso, Malacrino & Pistaferri 2020,
*Econometrica*; Merton 1968, *Science*) and of four not linked (Boghosian 2014, *Phys. Rev. E*; Li, Boghosian & Li
2019, *Physica A*; Gabaix 1999; Price 1976); the executive summary of the World Inequality Report 2022 (the poorest
half owns 2% of wealth and USD 4,100 per adult, the richest tenth 76%; **the report's 38% is the top 1%'s share of
the wealth added since the mid-1990s, not of the stock, and is not in the text**); Boghosian's article in
*Scientific American* (November 2019: Chakraborti's model of 2002, the affine wealth model matching US data "to
less than a sixth of a percent over a span of three decades", the proofs of 2015); the Tax Foundation's survey of
2024 (40.18% in 1980, 23.51% in 2024, unweighted averages of statutory rates); the Wikipedia pages linked (the
Russian Wikipedia has none for the yard-sale model or the Statute of Labourers: the English pages are used).
**Not checked**: that the name "yard sale" is Brian Hayes's ("Follow the Money", *American Scientist*, 2002: "the
name stuck the same year"); the details of the Italian model (1,000 agents, 40 years, talent normally distributed,
capital doubled by a lucky event with a probability proportional to talent and halved by an unlucky one); the
Norwegian data (2004–2015, returns rising with wealth); Pareto (an engineer with a railway company before
economics; the *Cours* of 1896–97 with tax data of several countries and centuries; "a fifth owns four fifths of the
land" is the usual attribution); the US census of 2020 (8.8, 3.9, 2.7 and 2.3 million); Zipf's rule for words; the
shape of citation counts; the richest fortune ("some hundreds of billions" of dollars, 2025); the top 1% wealth
shares in France and Britain (about 55–70% in 1910, about a fifth in the 1970s, after Piketty; the link is to the
book's Wikipedia page); the US top rates (7% in 1913, 94% in 1944–45, 91% in 1954–63); Scheidel (*The Great
Leveler*, 2017; Stanford; the four "horsemen"); the Black Death (1347–51, a third to a half of Europe; English real
wages about doubling by the fifteenth century; the Statute of Labourers of 1351); Scheve and Stasavage (*Taxing the
Rich*, 2016: twenty countries, two centuries, mass mobilisation), unnamed in the text; the agreement of October
2021 (about 140 countries, a 15% minimum) and the continuing bargaining over exemptions; that knowledge passes
between the makers of talking machines within months ("a year... six months" is Oleg's figure of speech, not a
measurement). The author's own: the comments under the SETI talk of June–July 2012 (the 999 out of 1,000; the 90%
and 45% example with ten countries and "five times"; "they will lower social benefits"), article 14 (the gap
between rich and poor among the components of self-destruction; fights for justice), a reader's objection under 14
(the beggar and the neighbour with three rams; the reader's billionaire is unnamed), a reader's insistence under 09
that some will profit while others starve, paraphrased without names. Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the
dream of the wages in matches, the count of the box, the forestry's prices and the bank's loan, the merchant family
of his town. Not used: the ergodicity economics of Ole Peters by name; the casino example of the *Scientific
American* article (win 20%, lose 17%); the Gini coefficient by name; Bouchaud and Mézard (2000); Simon's and
Price's models and "preferential attachment" by name; the Forbes 400 turnover (Arnott, Bernstein & Wu 2015: not
verified); "shirtsleeves to shirtsleeves in three generations" as a quotation; index funds and the "Big Three" as
today's largest holders (belongs to 55); the labour share of income; Piketty's r > g; land reform and the quiet
levelling of Latin America in the 2000s (only pointed at in "argued about"); wealth taxes of the 2020s.

**Figures**: `a-fifth-lost-a-fifth-won` (the two rows in the ash: 100 → 80 → 96 and 1,000 → 1,020 → 1,004),
`matches-go-to-one` (the curve of the real game on the computer: the richest hundred and the poorer five hundred
over a thousand rounds, with the one-match game as a dashed line), `a-brake-in-the-game` (bars: the same game with a
levy of one match in 2,000, 1,000, 200 and 100, and the world of 2021 for comparison; "a round is not a year").

**Left for later**: who holds the box at the end, the player that never gives back what it has received, and the
heaps with neither heirs nor funerals → 55 ("Who owns the tractor", still in the notebook; the line "Why money goes
to money" is crossed out); whether it is good if the lines of the machines run into one → 62 (49 gives the slope
and three brakes, and keeps "I don't know"); the row "one or several" of the portrait → 64; the three brakes among
the machines (owners who are people, several states, knowledge that leaks while chips do not) are candidates for a
dated mark at the check of 2028; the mutual need as a support against violence → 54 (not touched); dividends for
accumulated knowledge → 57 (not touched); Oleg's last doubt: "Who thought these rules up? Somebody did decide that
the stake is a share. — Nobody. It came out that way. — 'It came out.' And it is going to rain tomorrow. — What
is?" → 50.

## 50. What Is the "It" in "It Is Raining"

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 49 ("it is going to rain tomorrow. — What is?"). Oleg comes under an umbrella and
  brings the box back (forty matches). **The opening pun and the language examples are localised, and the two
  versions differ here on purpose**: in English the empty word is the "it" of "it rains" (candidates: the rain, the
  cloud, the weather; "the rain is raining" is "butter being buttery"); in Russian the rain "goes" ("Who has gone?
  Where?") and the empty place is the neuter ending of the one-word verb. The Russian title follows the plan's
  Russian version ("Who goes when the rain goes"). The number and order of replicas are the same (181 lines).
- Oleg's image: **the deputy director for general questions** ("the line was in the staff list, and a line in the
  staff list cannot stand empty"). The wind that is nowhere when it does not blow, the fire that is firewood when it
  does not burn: "the blowing blows", "the burning burns". **"One thing is happening, and the sentence wants two
  words for it: who, and what he does. So it cuts the happening in two and appoints one half the master of the
  other."** Three ways out of the rain: an empty word, a rain that walks, a single word whose form is still "made
  for a somebody" ("the deputy has only been hidden in the ending").
- **Oleg's first objection is the one he made in 02** ("poetry"; "Language is a convention. We agreed to speak so,
  as we agreed to drive on the right") and Andrey **gives way at once about the sun and the rain**: "you know
  better, and the phrase does you no harm. I want to see whether it is a convention where you do not know better."
- The film of 1944 is played on the log with the matchbox, a match and a pebble; Oleg, who watched Andrey's hand
  move them, says "the box is angry". Then the film itself (34 students, one wrote of triangles and a circle).
- **Gaika barks at the umbrella rolled by the wind** ("She thinks it is somebody. — Does she know grammar? — She
  knows 'sit' and 'no'"), and **Andrey corrects 02 openly**: "In 2011 I said that the habit comes in with the
  language... it is the other way about. The habit is older than the words." Babies of a year and the circle that
  jumps over a wall (1995); the rustle of 39 in three lines ("We come from the ones who jumped"; "the screw was
  turned all the way to 'somebody'"); Piaget's children; the pointed rocks; the hurried adults and the hurried
  physicists ("Trees give off oxygen so that animals can breathe": the lock with 38, "It is their waste"). **"School
  lays a path across the bed. The weed stays where it was. Give a man time and he walks on the path. Hurry him and he
  steps off it."**
- What is left of 02: "Half of it. Language is not where the habit comes from. Language is where it is cast in
  metal." Oleg's defect report (a casting with a cavity nobody can see until the cutter opens it; the column
  "culprit"; the control department does not take a report with an empty column; "The foreman of the shift. Or the
  turner who opened it up. Whoever stood nearest"; "Not one in forty years. The form does not provide for it").
  **"A sentence is that form. The column 'who' is printed in it beforehand, and it is not accepted empty... The old
  habit at least says something: 'there may be somebody there'. The form says nothing. It only has the column."**
  "You can't [argue with a column]. You can only fill it in." Language adds two things: it makes the hypothesis
  invisible ("not in what is said but in how") and hands it to every child whole, by the second road of 45 ("the
  alarm he gets with the drawings, the form with his first words").
- **Against himself**: the fire-insurance inspector of the 1930s (the drums marked "empty"; the people of Arizona
  said to have no words for time; the six-hundred-page book of 1983). "The strong claim, that a language is a prison
  for thought, did not stand." Physics in formulas with no column "who": **deferred to 56** ("one word in physics
  that I have owed you since the first" evening; the word "observer" is not said). The weak pull, measured: the two
  blues (the plan's "to retell at the table": "the one time our language got into a textbook on eyesight") and the
  fine for a verb ("he tore the costume" against "the costume tore": "By a third to a half").
- **What the column is for** (new in the cycle): the granddaughter's "It broke by itself" ("Then which of you
  described it more exactly? — She did. But I was not teaching her physics. If nobody broke it, nobody is going to
  hold the next one with both hands"). The lock with 43 and 47: "A book of debts cannot be kept without names... It
  is **the bookkeeping of a pack**: every deed is entered under a name, and the name answers for it. In a village
  the form is right nearly every time." One thing is taken out of the heap "not sorted" of 46: "The task is to keep
  accounts among those who are not kin. Our way is a 'who' in every sentence, the sentences about rain included."
- Between the cup and the rain: the crisis, the closed plant, money, "who started it"; Oleg's own "Who thought these
  rules up" of 49 ("it itched. As if you were covering for somebody. — You were holding a report with an empty
  column"). Thunder: "Sat somebody up there. With a hammer, or in a chariot" (one line, no religion argued; "they had
  our form and no physics"). **Why the series sounds gloomy**: "a story with no villain in it, no genius and no
  rescuer... does not go into the form. It feels unfinished, because the column is empty." Oleg: "there is nobody to
  take by the collar. Which is worse than gloomy."
- **The nearest column, "I"**: Descartes (1637), Lichtenberg ("one should say 'it thinks', as one says of a storm 'it
  lightens'... To assume the 'I' is a practical need"; the waste books; the figures of a spark in dust; "A man with a
  notebook"), "another German" a century later (even the "it" is too much), "a Viennese doctor" and "the It" that
  the English translators "dressed in Latin". The language's two sets of books: the official ("I decided") and the
  working ("it occurred to me", "it struck me. — Who struck?").
- **Oleg's "I raise my hand myself"** (the plan's): "Why the right one?... And why now? — Because you said that
  I..."; 04 in two lines; the changed photographs ("Three times out of four nobody noticed anything. And they
  explained"); "He filled the column in. After the event. Like my foreman in the report." The wrist of 1983 is given
  and **not leaned on**. **"The sentence 'I decided' is not a reading taken off an instrument. It is a report."**
  "'I' is a good column: it names the one who will answer for that hand tomorrow"; the lock with 35: "'I' is how a
  whirl is entered in the book of debts." **Where proof ends**: the photographs prove that the report can be wrong
  unnoticed; "that there is nobody at all to report... is my axiom from the evening about determinism. No experiment
  has shown it."
- **Oleg's strongest objection, against the author** ("your own weapon, handle first"): "The technosphere needs",
  "Nature set the setpoints", "Evolution moves to a new carrier": "You took my master away and seated your own."
  **Accepted** ("A fair blow. I speak this language and I have no other"), and a test is given: **can the sentence be
  said the long way, so that the "who" goes and nothing else is lost**. Three are unfolded (firms that put machines
  and people together outgrew the others, the selection with no breeder of 48; those with other setpoints left fewer
  children; the source of initiative of 04 as unfolded in 35). "Where it will not go the long way... I have smuggled
  a master in, and you catch me." **The question of 02 is not taken back** (why nature, being a "what", is refused a
  purpose and a mind without proof): "Today is its other half: the 'who' is granted his powers without proof as
  well. One ruler for both columns."
- **The machines are named by Oleg, after the middle**: "Those ones say 'I'... 'I'd take an umbrella if I were you.'
  If I were you!" "Nobody taught it that separately": in every sentence of ours the column is filled in; "It got the
  form together with the words, as your granddaughter did. Only without the alarm, without the cup and without the
  grandfather." The makers who looked inside while it added 36 and 59 (two paths, a rough sum and an exact last
  figure) and its account ("carried the one"): "It gave the report that people give. The card had been changed, and
  it explained the smile. I would not call it a lie: it has read ten thousand accounts of how adding is done, and
  not one of how it adds." Thousands of copies in one hour: the word "was made for a creature with one body and one
  line in the book of debts". **A guess, marked as one**: whether it will need a column of its own depends on whom it
  has to keep books with (copies are kin, 43; machines of different owners are strangers).
- **Not concluded that nobody is there**: "It says 'I': that came with the grammar and proves nothing. You feel that
  there is somebody behind the words: that is your alarm... and it proves nothing either. But from 'the word proves
  nothing' it does not follow that nobody is there." Two mistakes, as with the rustle; "With a snake we know the
  price of each. Here I do not." The question of 20 stays open (the lock with 31, "words prove nothing", is not
  linked in the dialogue: Andrey does not know that essay). Andrey thanks the machine: "A false alarm is cheap."
- **The plan's sentence**: "Today, I think, it is the most anthropocentric being on the Earth... It received our
  picture of the world without the world: every column filled in, and nothing to check the columns against." "A
  mind without a grandmother" (39) "with every grandmother's sayings by heart". What happens when it can feel the
  world not through us: **deferred to 58**.
- The questions asked about the machines ("Will it decide to get rid of us?", "Who will control it?", "Whose side is
  it on?"): "Who, who, whose. Three." "I am not saying the questions are empty. I am saying that the form asks them
  before we do" (the chain with no first link of 22).
- Strengths stated: solid (the triangles, the babies, the hurried physicists, the two blues and the fine for a verb,
  the changed photographs); refuted (that a language locks thought in, "and with it half of my own first evening,
  which I have corrected"); argued about (the wave before the wrist; how far a machine's account of itself can be
  trusted); Andrey's (the column as a pack's bookkeeping carried over to the rain; a story with no master turned down
  for its form; the machine's "I" came with the grammar; that there is no doer anywhere is his axiom). "— For once
  it is not gloomy. — It is not about the end. It is about the form the end is written on."
- Nobody says the forbidden word; eighteenth tick "again": "Came with an umbrella. Asked about the rain. Came out at
  those ones, and named them myself."

**Images and devices used**: the black umbrella, the notebook inside the jacket; the eleventh entry in the notebook
of dreams (the defect report with the empty column "culprit" and the pen that will not write; later "Into the income
column? — What did I ask you to think about before you went to sleep?... — A common cause. Two cocks. All right,
expense"); the fire lit with the third match; the candidates for the post; butter being buttery (three times); the
deputy director for general questions and the staff list; the granddaughter's wind that "had gone to sleep"; the
"it" in the sky with a watering can; driving on the right; the matchbox, the match and the pebble round the knot;
Oleg spitting into the fire ("The box"); the umbrella rolling on its ribs and Gaika; "sit" and "no"; the path
across the bed; "cast in metal"; the casting with a cavity; the welder and the empty drum; "Another of your
engineers"; the three blue squares; the cup and wet hands; "nobody to take by the collar"; the two sets of books;
the raised right hand with the pencil, lowered only after the photographs; the conjuror's trick; "handle first";
"smuggled a master in"; "One ruler for both columns"; "as your umbrella fits Gaika"; "thank you" to the machine;
"Count the columns"; the thunder ("It's thundering... — Don't. — I said nothing"); the lights of the settlement
going out ("They've cut the power. — Who has?... — A branch on the wire. I hope you are pleased").

**Facts used**: **from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for web checks to be kept to the critical ones);
**check before reusing**. Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref records of the seven DOIs linked (Heider &
Simmel 1944, *Am. J. Psychol.*; Gergely, Nádasdy, Csibra & Bíró 1995, *Cognition*, "Taking the intentional stance at
12 months of age"; Kelemen & Rosset 2009, *Cognition*; Kelemen, Rottman & Seston 2013, *J. Exp. Psychol.: General*;
Winawer, Witthoft, Frank, Wu, Wade & Boroditsky 2007, *PNAS*; Fausey & Boroditsky 2010, *Psychon. Bull. Rev.*;
Johansson, Hall, Sikström & Olsson 2005, *Science*); that the Wikipedia pages on Lichtenberg and Piaget exist in
both languages (through the API); that Anthropic's page "Tracing the thoughts of a large language model" (March
2025) answers and contains the sum 36 + 59. **Not checked**: Heider and Simmel's details (Smith College; cardboard
shapes on a glass plate shot frame by frame; 34 students, one of whom described the film in geometric terms; the
usual story of a bully, a couple and a house whose walls the large triangle breaks at the end); the 1995 design (a
small circle jumping over a barrier to a large one; longer looking at the jump once the barrier is gone); Piaget's
interviews (*The Child's Conception of the World*, 1926); Kelemen 1999 (children of seven and eight choosing "so
that animals wouldn't sit on them" for pointed rocks); the speeded condition of 2009 and 2013 (about 3.2 seconds;
the items about the sun and photosynthesis and about trees and oxygen; scientists accepted fewer such statements
than controls and more under speed); Whorf (a chemical engineer and fire-prevention inspector of the Hartford Fire
Insurance Company; the "empty" drums; the claim about Hopi, 1936–1940) and Malotki's *Hopi Time* (1983, about 680
pages), both unnamed in the text; Winawer's design (twenty blues, a triad of squares, the category advantage gone
under verbal interference with digits, none in English speakers; the size "a fraction of a second" is deliberately
vague); Fausey and Boroditsky's fines (the agentive wording raised them by about a half in one study and by about a
third among those who had also watched the video: given as "a third to a half"; the event, a costume torn at the
2004 Super Bowl show, is not named); Johansson's choice blindness (no more than about a quarter of the swaps
detected: "three times out of four"; the reasons quoted are of the kind reported, not quotations); Libet 1983 (the
readiness potential about 550 ms before the movement, the reported decision about 200 ms before; Schurger's 2012
objection is what "argued about" stands for), unnamed; Descartes's *Discourse* (1637); Lichtenberg (1742–1799,
Göttingen; the figures of 1777; the *Sudelbücher*, named after the merchants' waste book and printed after his
death; the entry "Es denkt, sollte man sagen, so wie man sagt: es blitzt", notebook K); Nietzsche (*Beyond Good and
Evil*, 1886, §17) and Freud's *Das Ich und das Es* (1923), the "Es" rendered as "id" in the English standard
edition, both unnamed (Oleg says "the id"; in the Russian version, "Freud's It", which is how it is translated into
Russian); the model's own account of the sum ("added the ones, carried the 1, then added the tens") against the
two parallel paths found inside (Claude 3.5 Haiku; the model and the company are not named in the text). Invented
for the frame (Oleg's side): the dream, the deputy director for general questions, the defect reports of the
foundry, the welder, the granddaughter's questions about the wind and the cup, the machine's advice about the
umbrella. Not used: Michotte's launching effect; the helper and the hinderer shown to babies (Hamlin, Wynn & Bloom
2007: later replications disagree); Fausey and Boroditsky's eyewitness study of 2011 (English against Spanish);
the split-brain "interpreter"; grammatical gender (used by the author in 02); the animate and inanimate nouns of
Russian; the word "observer" (56); ELIZA (21); people's politeness to chatbots and its cost; the names of Whorf,
Sapir, Nietzsche, Freud, Libet.

**Figures**: `two-triangles-and-a-circle` (a scheme of the film: what was on the screen and what was written),
`the-column-who` (the form with three columns; the first row differs by language: "it rains" and the Russian
one-word verb with a neuter ending), `two-words-for-blue` (a scheme of the 2007 experiment, not its data; the place
of the border on the strip is illustrative).

**Left for later**: the formulas with no column "who" and the word physics let back in → 56 ("a separate evening",
the debt of 02); what happens when the machine can feel the world not through us → 58 ("a separate evening"); the
feeling of freedom of a mind that models itself, and why the report cannot be checked from inside → 63 (not
touched: 50 says only that "I decided" is a report); whether the heir needs an "I" of its own (Andrey's guess: it
depends on whom it keeps books with) → the row of the portrait in 64; whether somebody is there behind the
machine's "I" stays open, as in 20 and 31; the three questions with a "who" in them ("Will it decide", "Who will
control it", "Whose side") → 54 and 55 (not touched); Oleg's last doubt, with the power cut by the thunderstorm:
"If those ones ever get out of hand, somebody will pull the plug out of the socket, and that will be that. There
is a 'who' for you, and with a hand. — If the light is not back by tomorrow, we shall count who hangs on that cord"
→ 51.

## 51. A Week Without a Socket

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 50 ("somebody will pull the plug... — we shall count who hangs on that cord"). The
  power has been out for a day (not the plan's two: the evenings are consecutive; the dispatcher promises a crew
  "tomorrow by dinner", "two days for one birch"). Oleg comes with a dead phone and opens with the technosphere
  himself ("A wind blew, and it is lying on its back. If need be, we pull the plug"), so the rule "Andrey is not
  the first to name them" holds; the talking machines are brought in by Oleg after the middle ("I said: those ones").
- **Oleg's own day, four items**: the borehole pump ("And the old well? — Filled in. In 2009... With my own hands. —
  One can go back... — ...and there is nowhere to go back to", the lock with 48), the freezer (his wife frying
  everything; "Gaika has had the best day of her life"), the shop (the card machine dead, Zina writes him down in a
  school exercise book: "The book. Between one's own", the lock with 47), the neighbour's generator and the filling
  station on the same line ("Ten tonnes of petrol under his feet, and the pump is electric").
- **The count by the clock for a town of a million** (a flat on the ninth floor), each step with a real blackout:
  the first minute and hours (lifts, lights, trains, tills; the mast on batteries: "the phone with a charge is a
  torch"; water and the flush) with 2003 ("A branch on a wire", the lock with the last line of 50; the alarm program
  that had hung an hour before); the second day (the refrigerator and the freezer) with Spain and Portugal 2025 (15
  gigawatts in five seconds; trains in the fields; cards, cash machines, battery radios; back by the next morning;
  **"And who put it back? — People"**, kept for the second half); the third day with Texas 2021 (gas needs
  electricity and electricity needs gas: "It hangs on its own cord"; 246 dead; four minutes and thirty-seven seconds;
  districts cut off by hand); the third and fourth day (three days on the shelves, "nine meals from anarchy"; a
  hospital's diesel for two to four days; dialysis; insulin from three firms).
- **A week is not counted**: "For a week I have no example, and I shan't invent one. Every case I have named was an
  island in a working sea... A week with no socket for everybody at once has never been tried." The title is the
  question Oleg asks ("And a week?"), not a scenario.
- **Oleg's objection (the plan's)**: people lived ten thousand years with no socket; his grandmother (born 1906, the
  wires came to the village in 1958, five children). "How many of them were there? — Five... — People. On the Earth."
  1.6 billion in 1900, a billion a hundred years earlier, over eight now. **The nitrogen**: about two kilograms in
  Oleg's eighty; the ocean of it overhead that nothing can take except some bacteria and lightning ("So yesterday's
  storm... — Dropped something on your potatoes. A pinch"); the old turnover (clover, lightning, the manure heap
  "which makes nothing new"); the 1898 warning; the laboratory table of 1909 and the plant of 1913; one or two percent
  of the world's energy; 48% (2008); about half the nitrogen in a body. Oleg: **"So a kilogram of me was made at a
  chemical plant... A lifetime in the control department, and it went past me with no stamp."** (the plan's "to
  retell at the table").
- **"Now your plug. Pull it, and those plants stop... the Earth feeds about four billion. — And we are eight. — Which
  four?"** Oleg's counter (half the grain goes into cattle, a third of the food into the bin: "your four turns into
  six") is **accepted as fair**: "the counts do differ... on a lean table the old turnover feeds more than four. But
  then you are taking away from everybody not the socket but the dinner, and by the mildest count you still subtract
  billions. And I have counted nitrogen alone."
- **The debt of 05 is paid** (why it is useless to destroy the technosphere): the author's 2011 half-answer under 05
  is quoted ("what is the sense of blowing up the iron if you go on using what it makes, the clothes, the shoes, the
  bread, the paper") and the other half is given: "It is not outside us, standing against us. A kilogram of you is
  it." The lock with 12: the machines subtracted from a loaf made it cost nearly three times the labour; "Subtract
  them from everybody at once, and what is subtracted is people." The Luddites in three lines ("Nobody argued them
  down. What are you wearing on your feet? — Socks. — Knitted by hand?").
- **Oleg's strongest objection** (the current one: an off switch for the AI, not for everything): "Leave the
  fertiliser plant alone... I said: those ones. The talking ones... a particular cable going in. Pull that one."
  **Conceded**: "On the talking machines, today, almost nobody hangs. Switch them all off tonight, and nobody goes
  hungry... Today that switch works. Is anybody's hand reaching for it? — Why should it? They do no harm." Then
  1913: nobody hung on the first plant; "Forty years later a billion hung on it, and there was no hand left... —
  Because the hand was hanging there too." **The rule of the switch: "A switch works while nothing hangs on the
  cord. And while nothing hangs on the cord, nobody has a reason to pull it."** Credited to "an Englishman" of 1980
  (Collingridge, unnamed; "I only hung the birch on it"). Oleg: "Two moves. And nobody makes the second."
- **How the cord is loaded**: the faulty update of July 2024 ("Nobody pulled anything. One file. And those were plain
  office computers"); Oleg's warehouse program (two days with a full warehouse, the storekeeper retired, her cards
  gone for scrap paper: "Not taken away. Not guarded", the lock with 42; "The chief accountant. It was cheaper than
  three storekeepers. — No villain. An accountant"); the same accountant hangs customers' answers, contracts and code
  on the talking ones; the plug has an owner with a rival (the 2023 letter of 27 in one line).
- **The author's 2017 argument, as the symmetry test**: the Kuzmins switched off for debts by the new meters with a
  relay ("The program saw the debt on the twenty-fifth, and the meter clicked. From town... — Whose hand? —
  Nobody's. The board passed the rule... I voted. For. Debts have to be paid"); the author's forum comment quoted
  (the non-payer who "usually goes round the rubbish heaps" and perhaps installed such a system himself; the control
  room with almost no people). **"The switch you are looking for exists. It works every day. It is set to switch
  people off, one at a time, and we set it so ourselves, with good intentions... It is called accounts
  receivable."** **Step six of 27 ("it stops giving") is pictured**: "You pictured a hand on a knife switch, as in
  your dream. I picture the Kuzmins' meter."
- **The other side of the switch is Oleg's**: "Who is coming to [the birch] tomorrow? Not a program. Tolik, with a
  chainsaw... Without Tolik your whole technosphere is lying on its back under a birch. So who hangs on whom?"
  Accepted as "the real one" and counted further: the finest chips from a handful of plants "nearly all on one
  island" (no politics), the machine that draws them from a single firm (about 180 tonnes, several cargo planes,
  hundreds of engineers for months), wafers that no hand touches and thousands of people who keep the plant alive, a
  chip that serves five years or so. "Within days the grids fall, because there is no Tolik. Within years the heads
  wear out, and no new ones are born. Today it can neither repair itself nor start itself after a fall." **Neither
  side can pull: the symbiosis of 11.**
- **The piece of the puzzle, as two questions**: (1) how long each lasts if the other is taken away: "Days for us;
  days for it"; (2) for how long each will go on needing the other: for us the term only grows ("In 1913, nobody hung
  on the plant. In 2008, half... — Who would pay for [making us need it less]? — Nobody"); for it "every need it has
  for a human hand is a line in somebody's costs. And thousands of engineers are paid to shorten that line" ("Tolik
  is a line"; drones inspect the lines, the birch is still his; the month without people of 27 and the sealed
  cylinder of 38 in a phrase each). **"Our dependence is measured in days, and it grows. Its dependence is measured
  in decades, and it shrinks... A clever machine that needs Tolik is a partner. The thing to watch is the second
  number."** "Decades" is marked as a forecast; **no new mark**: the mark of 27 is repeated (a data centre built with
  no workers on the site by the autumn of 2041, one in two). When the second number is nought "the switch has one
  side"; no date ("There will be no hand. There will be a rule, passed long before, by people who voted 'for'").
- Not advice (28). "All of us together, no: we are plugged in from the other end. Each for himself, yes. You can pull
  your own." The author's 2012 remark (those who live by their own muscles may last a generation or two longer) is
  given and **not taken for a way out**: it is the niche of 38, "room in it for a billion, with luck for a few. Not
  for eight. And one has to know how. Can you harness a horse? — I can shoe one. In a dream" (the dream of 45). "And
  would you go? — After one day with two buckets?... No. I shall wait for Tolik. — One can go back. — And nobody
  goes. I know."
- Strengths stated: solid (the three blackouts, the file of 2024, where the nitrogen comes from and when the plants
  appeared); an estimate, rough (the 48% and the half of the nitrogen in a body); argued about (how many the Earth
  feeds without the plants on a lean table); Andrey's (the sixth step as the Kuzmins' meter; decades, and shrinking);
  "The rule of the switch belongs to the Englishman." "— Gloomy. — Counted. In days and in kilograms."
- Nobody says the forbidden word; nineteenth tick "again": "Came with a dead phone. Was going to pull the plug. Found
  out I am hanging on the cord, a kilo of me for certain. And named those ones myself, in the first minute."
- The end: the crew comes a day early, the windows of the settlement come on. "There is your 'who', and with a hand.
  He has put the plug back in. — For now it is that way round."

**Images and devices used**: the phone laid on the log screen down; the twelfth entry in the notebook of dreams (the
knife switch the size of a door; the light going out in the grandmother's house; "From noon the instrument was off;
the afternoon does not count"); the birch by the pumping station; "lying on its back"; two buckets to the river; the
filled-in well; the pike and the New Year dumplings; Gaika sighing "with her whole belly"; Zina's exercise book
("Oleg, plot forty, bread, candles, two hundred and fifteen") and "the clay tablets of your Uruk"; the petrol under
the neighbour's feet; the flat on the ninth floor and the flush; "A terrorist. A meteorite. — A branch on a wire";
"It hangs on its own cord"; "By hand again"; nine meals; the island in a working sea; the grandmother's stove, cow
and paraffin lamp; "Eighty. Don't write it down"; the ocean of nitrogen; the pinch on the potatoes; Oleg looking at
his hand on both sides; "no stamp"; "Not at table"; "Which four?"; the socks; "a particular cable"; the son-in-law
who does a week's work in three days; the hand that hangs on the cord; the warehouse cards; "No villain. An
accountant"; the Kuzmins' meter; "accounts receivable"; Tolik with a chainsaw and a lift on a lorry; "Tolik is a
line"; "the wrong gauge"; the diesel growling beyond the birches and the yellow light crawling along the line; "One
day without light, and look what it does to a man."

**Facts used**: **all from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked again for web checks to be kept to the critical
ones); **check before reusing**. Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref record of Erisman, Sutton, Galloway,
Klimont & Winiwarter 2008 ("How a century of ammonia synthesis changed the world", *Nature Geoscience*,
doi:10.1038/ngeo325); that the Wikipedia pages linked exist in English and, through the API, in Russian (the
blackouts of 2003, 2021 and 2025, William Crookes, the Haber process, the Luddites, the CrowdStrike outage; the page
on the Collingridge dilemma has no Russian version, so the Russian text links to the English one). **Not checked**:
the Northeast blackout (14 August 2003, about 50 million people in eight states and Ontario; untrimmed trees under
sagging lines in Ohio; the alarm software of the Ohio utility's control room failed about an hour earlier; the
cascade took minutes; Cleveland's four pumping stations and more than a million people without water: the city is not
named; most supply restored in one to two days); the Iberian blackout (28 April 2025, 12:33; 15 GW lost in five
seconds, about 60% of demand: given as "more than half"; some 55–60 million people: "over fifty million"; about
35,000 passengers taken off stopped trains; mobile networks failing as the masts' batteries ran out; restoration by
the next morning by black start from hydroelectric stations and through the links with France and Morocco); Texas
(February 2021; more than 4.5 million homes and businesses; the state's final count of 246 deaths; the grid
operator's "four minutes and 37 seconds" from a collapse that could have lasted weeks or months; boil-water notices
for roughly half the state; the gas–electricity loop); the household figures (masts' batteries "a few hours"; a
refrigerator about 4 hours and a full freezer about 48; a hospital generator's fuel for two to four days; about
three days of stock in shops; the saying "nine meals from anarchy"; dialysis three times a week with more than a
hundred litres of treated water a session; three firms with about nine tenths of the world's insulin); world
population (about 1 billion in 1800, 1.6 billion in 1900, 8.2 billion in 2025); body nitrogen (about 2.6% by weight,
"about two kilograms" in eighty); the air (78% nitrogen: "four fifths"); nitrogen fixed by legume bacteria and by
lightning; Crookes's presidential address to the British Association (Bristol, 1898; unnamed in the text, linked);
Haber's demonstration of 1909 and the Oppau plant of 1913 (Bosch; both unnamed; about 200 atmospheres and 500 °C);
ammonia synthesis at 1–2% of the world's energy; Erisman's 48% for 2008 and the common statement that about half the
nitrogen in human tissue comes from the Haber–Bosch process (Smil's estimate of about 40% of mankind in 2000 is
not used; Smil is not named); "about four billion" without the plants (8.2 billion less 48%); the Luddites
(1811–1816, the stocking frames of Nottinghamshire; frame-breaking made a capital offence in 1812; seventeen
executed at York in January 1813: "more than a dozen in one town alone"); Collingridge (*The Social Control of
Technology*, 1980), unnamed; the outage of 19 July 2024 (about 8.5 million Windows computers, thousands of flights;
the firm is not named); the 2023 pause letter (from 27); chips (the most advanced nearly all made in Taiwan: "one
island", not named; EUV lithography machines from a single Dutch firm, about 180 tonnes, shipped in several cargo
planes, installed over months; overhead wafer transport; servers and accelerators serving about five years); drones
inspecting power lines; FANUC's month without people (27) and Project Natick (38), a phrase each. The author's
sources: the comment under 05 (the film; "Какой смысл взрывать железяки..."), the forum comments of 25 January 2017
(systems that cut people off for non-payment; the control room with almost no people), the 2012 comment on those
who live by their own muscle (from 27), article 12 (the loaf), article 11 (symbiosis). Invented for the frame
(Oleg's side): the dream, the borehole and the filled-in well, Zina and her exercise book, the neighbour's
generator, the grandmother (born 1906, electricity in 1958), the weight of eighty kilograms, the son-in-law, the
warehouse program and the card index, the Kuzmins and the meters with a relay, Tolik. Not used: the diesel in a loaf
(Smil's count); the guano islands by name; Haber and explosives; the cloud outage of October 2025; the experiments
with machines that edit their own shutdown script (22); hospital deaths in 2003; the Carrington storm of 1859;
"black start" as a term; the plan's "insulin on the third day" (insulin keeps for weeks without a refrigerator, so
the text speaks of where it is made).

**Figures**: `who-hangs-on-the-cord` (what stops in a town, by time: orders of magnitude, not a timetable),
`nitrogen-from-the-plant` (two bars and the body: the 48% of 2008 applied to the population of 2025, rough
arithmetic), `two-sided-switch` (the two questions asked of both sides; "decades" is Andrey's forecast).

**Left for later**: where the former masters go when they are no longer needed, and the table of fates (the niche of
38, the organelle of 42, the livestock and pet of 48) → 52 (Oleg's last doubt: "Then we go the way of the
dinosaurs. I am not asking. I am saying. — And what became of the dinosaurs? — They died out... — Come before dark
tomorrow. I shall show you a few... They are in my apple tree every evening"; the opening line of the plan, "Do you
know whom I feed all winter? Dinosaurs", is left for 52); "then let it go to space and leave the Earth to us" and
resettlement → 61 (not touched); the hand that does not reach for the switch because "they do no harm" as a matter
of law and control → 54 and 55 (not touched); the second number as a gauge: 51 names it and repeats one mark of 27;
new marks with odds belong to 53, 60 and 64; how step six looks in detail was only pictured (the meter), with no
date, as in 27.

## 52. Dinosaurs at the Feeder (the second assembly)

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 51 ("Then we go the way of the dinosaurs... — Come before dark tomorrow. I shall show
  you a few"). Oleg comes before dark; Andrey is pouring sunflower seeds into a feeder cut out of a milk carton in
  the apple tree. The plan's epigraph is said at once ("Do you know whom I feed all winter? Dinosaurs. In summer
  they come out of habit"). A great tit ("Seventeen grams") takes a seed. The fire is lit only in the middle of the
  evening, when it gets dark and the tits have gone.
- **The descent**: "what is a dinosaur?" (a lizard the size of a house, cold blood, died out); "Is a whale a fish?
  — One does not go by the looks"; Oleg's control department goes by the route sheet and the number of the melt
  stamped on a casting: "So by where it comes from." **The bird sits inside the family tree of the dinosaurs**, on
  the branch of the two-legged hunters, "closer kin to the tyrannosaur than the tyrannosaur is to the three-horned
  one". "It is stamped on her": the wishbone (yesterday's chicken from the freezer of 51; the bone that "for a
  hundred years stood between the birds and the dinosaurs": the Bavarian fossil of the 1860s, the Englishman, the
  collarbones the dinosaurs were thought to lack), hollow bones, three toes, the scales on her legs, the nest (**the
  "egg thief" of 1923 that for seventy years was listed as a thief "for sitting on its own eggs"**), the feathers
  (the metre-long one of 1996 with a fringe of down; dozens since, one with four wings, a fluffy nine-metre relative
  of the tyrannosaur; the grains of pigment: "ginger, with a striped tail").
- They "were masters of the land for a hundred and sixty million years"; the birds split off about 150 million
  years ago and lived "beside the big ones". **The stone** (66 million years ago, ten kilometres; the metal in the
  layer of clay, 1980; the crater of 180 km found later). "The blow killed those who were near. The rest were killed
  by the dark" (a year or two, "the estimates differ"). Yesterday's question made larger: who in the settlement gets
  through a year without sun? "Whoever has a cellar." On dry land "almost nothing heavier than a dog like Gaika".
  Most birds died too (the tree-dwellers "went with the trees"); every bird alive descends from a few that lived on
  the ground (2018), small, with a beak and no teeth; "A seed is a tin... The forest is dead, and its pantry is
  full" (2016). **"Not the strongest... not the cleverest. The one that needed little and could eat what keeps."**
  Oleg: "My grandmother. Yesterday's."
- The plan's line to retell: about 11,000 kinds of birds against about 6,500 of mammals ("the figures depend on who
  does the counting, the order does not"); **"By the number of kinds, the age of the dinosaurs is still going on.
  We call them birds."**
- **Andrey corrects article 07** (openly): in 2011 he said "people will share the fate of the dinosaurs, and have to
  clear the road" and meant extinction. "The sentence I do not take back. But I had not looked up what that fate
  was. I said 'fate' and saw a skeleton in a museum. And it was sitting in my apple tree." Oleg: "Convenient...
  the gloomy prophecy turns out to mean birdsong. — Wait with the birdsong. Ask what was kept and what was paid."
- **The page "Where the former ones went" is completed.** Oleg reads the three rows as written in 38, 42 and 48,
  then writes: (4) **"A descendant: kept the line. Paid with the size, the role and the name"** ("the tyrannosaur
  is nobody's grandfather": the line is a small side branch; the name: "What did I say when I came? 'That is a
  tit.'"; "That row hangs on nobody... Take the carton away, and she will live"); (5) **"Fuel"**: kept nothing,
  paid everything, "What has been burned is not asked" (the coal forest; "two rows for one master": the horsetail
  in Oleg's potato patch and the club moss under the firs); (6) under a line, **"A print in stone"** (see below).
  The order on the page is niche, organelle, livestock and pet, descendant, fuel, print (the plan had fuel before
  the descendant).
- **The niche read from the other end**: "where were we all that time? — Under their feet", the size of a shrew,
  out at night; the eyes still pay (two of four sensors of colour lost, one got back by the apes; "A patch", the
  lock with 46). "Who sits in a niche? — The former ones. — And the next ones." Oleg: "Then one can sit it out."
  **"A niche is a ticket to a lottery in which the draw is a stone from the sky"** (the anaerobes have been waiting
  2.4 billion years).
- **Oleg's first objection (the continuation's, survivorship)**: "The table is too good... Where is the row of those
  who simply died?... ninety-nine of a hundred... You are showing me the batch that passed inspection... It is the
  dream book again." Answered in two parts. (1) Ninety-nine of a hundred kinds, yes: a kind lasts a million years or
  a few, and its chance of dying out does not depend on its age (1973): "Like a glass in the kitchen. Like a firm"
  (the lock with 40); "The kind dies, the branch goes on". "Will the kind called man last for ever? — No" (300,000
  years old); the author's 2012 question about the indulgence "that the dinosaurs were not given". (2) **Conceded**:
  whole branches go (the trilobites; with the stone the flying lizards, "the second wing of the four", the sea
  lizards, the ammonites). **"I have no rule for who lands in that row."** The sixth row is written "under a line.
  I cannot cross it out, and I shall not pretend. I say only that it is shorter than the school book made it."
- **The assembly proper** (what no single evening held). (a) The column "paid" read downwards by Oleg: in the four
  rows that did not pay everything "it is one and the same thing. They no longer decide. What the air will be. What
  to want. Who is born. Who is reckoned with": **"the right of signature"** ("A man who is moved aside keeps his
  office, his ration and the name on the door. He loses the signature"); Andrey says it "the long way" (50). (b) The
  small print of the column "kept": "while the job is needed", "while the feeder wants", and a new one added to the
  first row, **"while the place is not needed"**; so **"three rows hang on the new master's cord"** (he does not go
  there, cannot do it himself, wants it), one he burns, and one hangs on nobody: "The tit and the horsetail. The
  ones that need little." (c) **A former master is not one thing with one fate**: the dinosaurs sit in three rows
  (the tit, yesterday's chicken as livestock, the neighbour's parrot as a pet, the big ones under the line), the
  bacteria in two and "masters to this day, in every leaf", the coal forest in two. So Oleg's sentence of 51 "is
  worthless... Which one?", and **"will mankind survive" is badly posed: "which part of it, into which row, and on
  what that row hangs".**
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**: he draws the column "us" himself ("Since you are
  sitting with your mouth shut again... the new master in it is those ones. I am saying it"). The niche: the
  grandmother and yesterday's billion; whether the place will be needed is the author's 2012 forecast (38), "I have
  no fact". The organelle: Tolik and the customer who wants; "your second number of yesterday. Decades, and
  shrinking". Livestock and pets: "Fed on schedule... the pension comes on the fifth... Then I am a pet of the
  pension fund"; "while the feeder wants" is the button of 48, "still a question". Fuel: "They do not burn us. They
  have watts enough... They were raised on something. On everything people have written in five thousand years.
  There is your seam"; Andrey: the move of the drawings of 42 "seen from the boiler room. With one difference: coal
  burns once. A seam of words can be burned as many times as you like. — That is no comfort to the tree."
- **Oleg's card, "they are our children"** (the reader under 12, never answered; the great-nephew's words). Andrey
  quotes his own 2012 consolation and its continuation (no more in common with people than people have with a
  slipper animalcule; "with a very great wish one can pass for the parent of a super-child"). Counted by the three
  roads of 45: no genes; the second road, through the straw ("Semyonych's 'it sings wrong' did not get in"); images
  (19), "laid into a thing that then goes on by the third". **Conceded: a descendant it is ("nature does not care
  what the line is written on"). "But see which part of us takes that row. The part that was written down. Our
  words go into the descendant. We do not."**
- **Oleg's counter (the continuation's, left partly open)**: "My grandchildren are not me either... Why is a quarter
  a consolation, and a library is not?" Andrey: "A good question, and I have no arithmetic for it"; the consolation
  comes from a button and not from the quarter; "Yours, I see, does answer. — A little." Two things for the notebook:
  the price of the row ("They will not be called people"; the side branch "is what we wrote down") and what a child
  owes: **the author's 2012 line, we are the super-children of the single-celled and poison them with antibiotics;
  "Kinship is a fact about where a line came from. It is not a promise."** "The tit brings no flowers to the
  museum." One difference granted: "The tit does not know that she is a dinosaur. Ask one of those about people, and
  now and then it says 'we'"; "Whether that is kinship or an accent, I do not know."
- **Oleg's strongest objection (the plan's), deferred as planned**: not one of them understood; "We do... Whoever
  knows the rows beforehand can bargain over the row. Or at least choose." Andrey: "the strongest thing said this
  evening, and I shall not close it tonight"; against (the author's 2011 line on the dinosaurs who "had not the wit
  to hasten their exit" by quarrels, people manage easily), for (**"there is no precedent. Not one of the former
  masters could read"**). "It wants an evening of its own... nearer the end of the summer". Oleg writes **"To
  bargain"** in capitals with a question mark on the last page.
- Two points against the table, said by Andrey: every row took millions of years and the horse took forty ("the
  rows may be the same and the clock quite another"); "a cell kept its lodger because it had no drawing board. This
  master has one", so the second row "may come out shorter than it reads". The row under the line is not crossed
  out for us either (14). "One man in several rows. I have counted myself into three."
- Strengths stated: solid (birds are dinosaurs; the stone and its date; the survivors small and ground-living; the
  counts of kinds; the age of coal; the chickens); "a reconstruction, and a good one" (the seeds); argued about (why
  the old forest did not rot); Andrey's (the page as a whole; the price as the signature; several rows and not one;
  "our children" means our words); "a list of what has already happened, not of what can happen". **"— Gloomy. —
  Go and look into the apple tree in the morning. She does not find it gloomy. — She does not know that she is a
  dinosaur. — From inside, no row looks like a fate. It looks like a morning."**
- Nobody says the forbidden word; twentieth tick "again": "Came to look at dinosaurs. Came out at which part of me
  goes into which row. Drew the column myself, and named those ones myself."

**Images and devices used**: the thirteenth entry in the notebook of dreams (the museum skeleton as long as the
assembly shop, the label "Do not feed", the pass with the skeleton's photograph; nobody rang); the milk-carton
feeder; the black tie of the tit and the seed pinned under its toes; the route sheet and the number of the melt;
the three-horned one "in your granddaughter's book"; the wishbone pulled with the granddaughter ("She won"); the
department "stripped of the bonus" for a label; the ginger dinosaur with a striped tail; the cellar, the haystack
and "meat by the hundredweight"; Gaika thumping her tail at her name; "A seed is a tin"; the skeleton in a museum
and the apple tree; "birdsong"; the bent finger; the shrew, the rat and the badger; the lottery ticket; "The sun of
the last sixty years"; bark like a snake's skin; the horsetail that comes up after liming ("No."); the batch that
passed inspection; the glass in the kitchen; the indulgence; shells "coiled like a ram's horn"; "nobody even burns
them"; reading the page "not along, but down"; the office, the ration and the name on the door; the cord (51); the
neighbour's parrot; the line drawn down the right-hand edge of the page; the pet of the pension fund; the seam and
the boiler room; forty years of inspection reports; "Fuel: possibly"; a quarter and a library; the tablets for the
throat in March; flowers to the museum; "two old men, drawing the table before the event"; the notebook held up to
the fire; the teaser (the academician in the pocket, "Top marks in physics for the term", "The academician is
there. The pot is the same", the pocket "lying on the table, screen down", "a tick of your own. The first of the
summer").

**Facts used**: **nearly all from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked again for web checks to be kept to the
critical ones); **check before reusing**. Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref records of Field, Bercovici,
Berv, Dunn, Fastovsky, Lyson, Vajda & Gauthier 2018 ("Early Evolution of Modern Birds Structured by Global Forest
Collapse at the End-Cretaceous Mass Extinction", *Current Biology*, doi:10.1016/j.cub.2018.04.062) and of Larson,
Brown & Evans 2016 ("Dental Disparity and Ecological Stability in Bird-like Dinosaurs prior to the End-Cretaceous
Mass Extinction", *Current Biology*, doi:10.1016/j.cub.2016.03.039); that the Wikipedia pages linked exist in
English and, through the API, in Russian (Archaeopteryx, Oviraptor, Sinosauropteryx, the Chicxulub crater, the
Carboniferous, the trilobites; the Russian page for the Red Queen hypothesis is titled after the "Black Queen" of
the Russian translation of Carroll; there is no Russian page for the nocturnal bottleneck, so the Russian text
links to the English one). **Not checked**: the great tit's weight (16–21 g: "seventeen grams"); a large
*Tyrannosaurus* at about 8 tonnes; birds as theropod (saurischian) dinosaurs and *Triceratops* as an ornithischian;
*Archaeopteryx* (Solnhofen, 1861, about 150 million years old, "the size of a magpie"); Huxley's proposal of the
1860s (unnamed); Heilmann's argument of 1926 from the supposedly missing collarbones and the later finds of
wishbones in theropods, *Tyrannosaurus* included (the century is given as "a hundred years", loosely); the
*Oviraptor* of the American Museum expedition (Gobi, 1923, named 1924; the embryo of 1993–94 and the brooding
skeleton of 1995: "seventy years"); *Sinosauropteryx* (Liaoning, 1996, about a metre; its colour from melanosomes,
2010: chestnut with a banded tail, given as "ginger"); *Microraptor* (four wings) and *Yutyrannus* (2012, about nine
metres), unnamed; dinosaurs as the dominant land animals for roughly 160 million years (the author's figure, used
in 45); the Chicxulub impact (about 66 million years ago, a body of about 10 km, a crater of about 180 km buried
under roughly a kilometre of rock; Luis and Walter Alvarez and the iridium layer, 1980, unnamed; the crater
identified about 1990–91); darkness for months to about two years; about three quarters of species lost (not
stated in the text); no land animal much above 25 kg surviving ("a dog like Gaika"); the enantiornithine birds as
the most diverse birds of the Cretaceous, many toothed, all extinct; Field's reconstruction (forest collapse from
pollen and spores, the fern spike; ground-dwelling ancestors of all living birds); Larson's seed hypothesis; seeds
lasting years in the soil; about 11,000 species of birds (IOC) and about 6,500 of mammals; the first mammals small
and nocturnal through the Mesozoic; the nocturnal bottleneck (two of four cone pigments lost; a third regained in
Old World primates; birds have four); the Great Oxidation Event at 2.4 billion years (38); coal at about a third of
the world's electricity in 2024 and still the largest single source, about 9 billion tonnes mined a year, coke in
steelmaking; the Carboniferous (about 359–299 million years ago), its scale trees of thirty metres and more with
diamond-patterned bark, the tree horsetails of about ten metres; the two accounts of why the wood did not rot
(fungi that digest lignin appeared later: Floudas and others, 2012; against it, Nelsen and others, 2016), both
unnamed; *Equisetum* as the last genus of its group and the club mosses as relatives of the scale trees (the
quillworts are closer, not mentioned); "99% of all species that ever lived are extinct" (Raup); species lasting
about one to ten million years; Van Valen's law of constant extinction (1973), unnamed; *Homo sapiens* at about
300,000 years; the trilobites (about 521–252 million years ago, more than 20,000 described species, gone in the
end-Permian extinction); pterosaurs, marine reptiles and ammonites lost at the end of the Cretaceous; about a
thousand named kinds of non-bird dinosaurs; more than 25 billion chickens alive at any moment (FAO) and poultry at
about 70% of the biomass of all birds (Bar-On, Phillips & Milo, 2018); language models that say "we" about people
(an observation, not a study). The author's sources: article 07 (Andrey: "А людей постигнет участь динозавров...
должны освободить дорогу"); the comment under 06 of 23 November 2011 ("У динозавров не хватило ума ускорить
подобным образом свой сход со сцены, а вот у людей — запросто"); the seminar thread, 27 June 2012 ("человечеству
выдана индульгенция, которой не было у динозавров?"; "Динозавры тоже были ценны сами для себя") and 30 October
2012 ("Возможно, кого-то успокоит мысль о том, что новая эволюционная форма материи является наследником
человечества... сойти за родителя супер-дитяти... мы — тоже супердитяти тех самых одноклеточных, которых
теперь... травим антибиотиками"); the reader under 12 of 16 November 2011 (the technosphere as children who
outlive their parents; paraphrased, unnamed); the 2012 forecast on the environment (from 38). Invented for the
frame (Oleg's side): the dream, the granddaughter's book and the wishbone, the horsetail in the potato patch, the
neighbour's parrot, the pension on the fifth, the forty years of inspection reports, the tablets in March, the
great-nephew's top marks in physics. Not used: the collagen recovered from a tyrannosaur bone; medullary bone;
*Mei long* asleep like a bird; the tyrannosaur living nearer in time to us than to the stegosaur; ultraviolet in
the blue tit's cap; the giant dragonflies of the Carboniferous; "oil is made of dinosaurs" (it is not; left for
another time); limestone and cement as "a house of shells"; the Deccan volcanism; crocodiles and turtles as
survivors; the Neanderthals and other human kinds (a possible seventh row, "absorbed": a few percent of their genes
live in us; left for the evenings near the end); the horse numbers of 34.

**Figures**: `dinosaurs-still-here` (a scheme: only the order of branching and two dates are to scale; two bars of
the counts of kinds), `where-the-former-ones-went` (the table of fates with the column "it lasts"; rows 1–3 from 38,
42 and 48, the page as a whole is Andrey's reading), `which-part-into-which-row` (Oleg's column "us": a reading,
not data and not a dated forecast).

**Left for later**: "To bargain?" (whether knowing the rows beforehand gives a say in the row; "there is no
precedent") → 65 and 66, written on the last page of the notebook as Oleg's; why a quarter of one's drawings
consoles and a library does not ("I have no arithmetic for it") → 65, or left open; whether the heir's "we" is
kinship or an accent → 64 (the row "relation to us" of the portrait); "will the place be needed" (the niche) and
the technosphere leaving for space → 61; the button, still a question (48); the personal academician and the pot
→ 53 (Oleg's last doubt: "One thing your page has not got. A former master who learns from the new one... Perhaps
we shall grow. — The academician is there. The pot is the same... Ask the boy tomorrow to solve one problem with
the pocket lying on the table, screen down"); the third assembly (the portrait of the heir) → 64.

## 53. A Million Mendeleevs

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 52 ("The academician is there. The pot is the same... Ask the boy tomorrow to solve
  one problem with the pocket lying on the table, screen down"). Oleg comes with his school problem book of the
  ninth form and the result of a video call at noon: **"One all."** A block on a slope with friction: eight
  minutes, and the boy explained it "so that I understood, which is more than our physics master managed in 1981".
  A ladder against a wall: forty minutes, not solved ("he looked at the pocket the way Gaika looks at a sausage");
  with the screen up, two minutes. Oleg claims **half a tick of his own**; Andrey: "Put it down. In pencil...
  Because we have not yet agreed what it was that the academician filled."
- **Oleg's thesis (the plan's "best objection")**: the ceiling of 11 "was a ceiling of teachers" (one for thirty
  heads, with a cold, a queue at the door); now every head has its own, never tired, "at three in the morning".
  "It has been taken off." Andrey: "the best thing that can be said against the eleventh evening. And I owe on it":
  in 2012 nobody got out of him what would make him take the limit back (the open thread of the seminar note).
- **The pot and the liquid** (the author's 2013 image; the forum debt). Descent through the ladle of Oleg's foundry
  ("Five tonnes... Can there be six? — There can. On the floor"; filled twice as fast it is "full twice as soon.
  Five"). The capacitor: farads and coulombs. **"Intelligence is the capacity. Mind is what has been poured in."**
  The refinement of 16's one line ("intelligence is the ceiling, mind fills it" was "half the truth"): "A ceiling
  is what that number looks like from a full pot. From an empty one the same number is a promise" (the author's
  "potential when nearly empty, limit when nearly full"); a "gifted" child and "an old man who is full" (44) may
  have the same number. The farads are stamped nowhere: "we see only the liquid". The author's twins (one at home
  and through an institute, one in a wolf's den): the same intelligence, a different mind.
- **"The liquid comes only from outside, and only on time."** The girl found in Los Angeles in 1970 at thirteen
  (words by the hundred, sentences not); "so as not to lean on one unhappy child", the deaf signers who met sign
  language at four or at twelve and after thirty years of daily use still differ (1990). Oleg: "The kitten's eye.
  The window" (44). **Andrey corrects article 11**: "their memory was already taken" was "roughly said: the window,
  it seems, shuts by the calendar". "No teacher pours into a pot whose hour has passed."
- **What the academician is for**: to reach "the edge", where nobody has been; Oleg "got to the plant" (fifteen
  years; the boy till thirty: the doubt deferred from 40 is taken up). Ages: Mendeleev thirty-five ("Old. With that
  beard"), Newton twenty-three, Einstein twenty-six, Dirac twenty-five; the author's 2012 question about knowing
  Dirac's equation at three. **"Personal academicians are not new"**: the prince who had Aristotle from thirteen
  ("He conquered. Half the world. — And died at thirty-two"); the Englishman born in 1806 (Mill, unnamed; Greek at
  three, Latin at eight; "an advantage of a quarter of a century"; broke down at twenty; the first great book at
  thirty-six): **"The teacher poured faster. The book did not come sooner."** ("One Englishman. — One.")
- **The count** (Jones 2009): 55,000 American inventors with known birthdays, patents of 1975–1999: the age at a
  first patent up by two thirds of a year a decade; inventors per patent from 1.73 to 2.33; the lone inventor
  changes field less and less often; "the death of the Renaissance man". Jones 2010: the age of the great work up
  by about six years over the twentieth century, "and the years are lost at the beginning" (about eight years
  later). **Oleg's objection (the continuation's)**: "That is not a pot, that is a queue" (44: the chair is taken,
  the grant comes past forty). Answer: "For a patent nobody asks leave: an idea and a fee. And a queue would eat
  the years in the middle. They are gone from the start, where the school is."
- **A million Mendeleevs** (the author, 2012; the title). Oleg: take more heads, "Not one Mendeleev: a million."
  Why two or three: each of the million first has to "carry in everything that lies before the edge. His own goes
  on top, into what room is left... the common part is counted a million times and the room on top once each."
  The real Mendeleev "had room" (oil, the balloon, weights and measures, the cheese dairies on the day of the
  table, suitcases made with his own hands); move the edge 150 years on and "what is left on top is a thimble".
  Oleg supplies the strip ("an engineer for gating systems"). **The author's 2017 line: narrow specialisation is
  litmus paper of a system in saturation. "A full pot does not look like stupidity. It looks like a specialist."**
  Teams as "capacitors in parallel: the farads add" (the 2015 paper with 5,154 authors, "nine pages of physics and
  twenty-four pages of names"; "At the plant that was called the payroll"); the wires between the pots are the 39
  bits of 45.
- **The bill** (the plan's line to retell): Moore's law since 1971; by 2014 the doubling took **eighteen times more
  researchers** than in the early seventies; over the American economy the yield of a researcher halves every
  thirteen years. Oleg: "The squirrel in the wheel" (40). "There we saw that the inventions have to come sooner
  every time. Here is the count of who has to make them."
- **The exposure of anthropocentrism**. Oleg: the low apples were picked, "ideas are harder to find". "Harder for
  whom?... 'High' is not a property of the apple. It is the apple measured against a ladder. 'The fruit hangs
  higher' and 'the ladder is short' are one sentence, said from two ends. And we always say it from the apple's
  end. — Because the ladder is us." The dog's pot ("A decent one"; she will not learn the multiplication table),
  the fly, "And a man?... I was going to say yes"; the author's question about the six thousand languages.
  **"Everybody has a pot: the fly, the dog, the neighbour. And ours has no bottom."** The weed of 02 "has come up
  in a new pot. 'Man can learn anything, he was only badly taught.' It sounds like a complaint about teachers."
- **Oleg's objection (the continuation's): the pot grows**, test scores rise with every generation. About three
  points a decade through the twentieth century; in Norway, for conscripts born after 1975, the score goes down,
  and inside one family (2018): "Brothers get their pots from the same parents. — So it was the liquid." "A pot can
  be dented by hunger, and the dent can be beaten out. That is not growth."
- **The machine teacher, both results** (as the plan asks). Chicago, 1984: the tutored average pupil above 98 in
  100 of a class of thirty, called "a problem and not a result"; later counts of live tutors "under half of that".
  Turkey (2025): textbook, the machine as it comes, the machine as a teacher; practice +48% and +127%; the
  examination with screens down −17% and "the same as the textbook. Not worse. And not better." Harvard (2025): 194
  students, a tutor built by the lecturers, a crossover with a live class: more than twice the gain, 49 minutes
  against an hour. "So which of them is right? — Both." Oleg: "In Turkey the machine solved for them. At Harvard it
  made them solve." **"To gnaw or to ask" (48). "The liquid goes in only through the gnawing. When he asks, it is
  poured past the pot."** "The slope he had gnawed at some time. The ladder he had asked."
- **What the academician changed**: one of the three narrow places of 45. The mouth is "wide now, and patient";
  the ear (39 bits) and the growing of the skill with a night's sleep (33) are the same. "It pours faster, where
  the pupil lets it."
- **The debt of 2012 paid: what would refute the limit.** "Suppose it is faster all the way... and a third of the
  time is saved. Then your boy stands at the edge not at thirty but at twenty... ten years gained are a century and
  a half" of the edge's retreat. **"If it comes to pass, I take the eleventh evening back. Not for ever, the pot is
  the same. For a long time."** Why he thinks not, "of different strength": measured (most will ask, "a skill that
  is not needed is not guarded", 42); one Englishman; and a question with no answer: **"A mind ages by filling (44).
  Pour twice as fast, and when is the pot full? — At thirty... An old man of thirty. — I don't know. Nobody has
  measured that."**
- **Oleg's strongest objection (the plan's)**: do not walk to the edge, "let the pocket remember the road"; a man
  keeps "the thimble: the idea, the question". Answer as planned: "Then who made the discovery?" The author's 2013
  capacitors (one microfarad in parallel with a hundred: "A hundred and one... The pair's... who needs whom?");
  **the discovery was made by the system "man and machine", and the shares are counted as in 12.** Oleg: "For now
  the one per cent is the one who asks the question. The customer" (22). **The test "the screen down"**: "What he
  can explain with the screen down is in his pot. What he cannot is in the pair's."
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle** ("Those ones. Do they have a pot?"). The author's 2013
  answer: "intelligence is the limit... the technosphere has one too and always will. The only question is whether
  it is larger than ours." Three differences, all from earlier evenings: filled once and copied (26); redrawn every
  year or two, "there is a drawing board" (52); "theirs fills too" (the loss of plasticity of 44). Oleg's card: "a
  country of geniuses in a server room... your million Mendeleevs. Sitting in my foundry." Andrey: "a million
  identical Mendeleevs know what one knows" and "a copy with no search is a stop" (45), against them; for them,
  **"Our million carried in the common part a million times, thirty years each. Theirs carry it in once"**, and can
  go "from the same edge in a million directions". Whether anything new is found so, "and who will understand it",
  is deferred to an evening of its own (60); Oleg: "I'll bring it."
- **Two new marks** (the first of Part VI; written by Oleg under the half tick). (1) Slow: the age at a first
  invention, counted as in 2009, "for work done by a head, with the screen down", **will not turn down by the
  autumn of 2041, three to one**; if it turns down only with the screen up, the discovery is the pair's; "this
  needle grows muddier every year. Soon there will be nobody at the edge with the screen down." (2) Fast: the
  international test of fifteen-year-olds in mathematics (PISA, unnamed; 463 in 2025, the lowest on record, 472 in
  2022): **by the autumn of 2036 no round will have come out above the level of 2022, two to one**; if one does,
  "ink over the pencil. A whole tick, and yours." "The needle is dirty... — Dirty. I have no cleaner one."
- Strengths stated: solid (the count of inventors; the eighteen times; the two experiments "each as far as it goes,
  one school and one course"; the deaf signers; the Norwegian brothers); argued about (why the age goes up: the
  economists speak of the burden of knowledge and "do not say that the pot is near the brim"); the author's (the
  pot and the liquid, the million Mendeleevs, specialisation as litmus paper); "a reading and not a measurement:
  that we are near the brim. Nobody has seen the brim"; a question (whether a pot filled faster grows old sooner).
  "And what do I tell the boy? — Nothing. Ask him tomorrow to explain the ladder to you. With the screen down."
- Nobody says the forbidden word; twenty-first tick "again": "Came with a problem book, to show that we shall grow.
