  Came out at who needs whom, in microfarads. And put those ones into my foundry myself." Oleg's own half tick stays
  in pencil until the mark of 2036.

**Images and devices used**: the fourteenth entry in the notebook of dreams (the blank examination ticket, the
great-nephew as examiner, "ask it yourself"; nobody rang); the problem book without a cover; "One all"; the slope
and the ski jump; the pocket and the sausage; half a tick in pencil; the teacher with a cold; "Another pot? We
boiled one in the spring"; the pot drawn in the ash; the five-tonne ladle and the sixth tonne on the floor; farads
"stamped on a man"; the twins; "With that beard"; the princes; the quarter of a century; "an idea and a fee"; the
room on top and the thimble; the cheese dairies and the suitcases; the engineer for gating systems; the transistor
that has nothing more to give; the payroll; the apple and the stepladder; Gaika's one standing ear; the fly ("Stop
it"); the six thousand languages; the weed in a new pot; the dent beaten out; "Solved. In the pocket"; poured past
the pot; one out of three; an old man of thirty; one microfarad and a hundred; the foundry full of Mendeleevs;
ink over the pencil; the dirty needle; the teaser (the clever with long teeth, "It was not fools who made the
bomb", the dove with a twig in its beak, "Have you ever seen doves fight? — Doves? They coo").

**Facts used**. **Checked on the day of writing against the papers**: Jones, "The Burden of Knowledge and the
'Death of the Renaissance Man'" (*Review of Economic Studies*, 2009, doi:10.1111/j.1467-937X.2008.00531.x: ages of
55,000 inventors, 2.1 million US patents of 1975–1999, age at first innovation +0.66 years per decade for those
first seen at 25–35 after 1985, team size 1.73 → 2.33, the probability of switching field falling by about 6% a
decade); Jones, "Age and Great Invention" (*Review of Economics and Statistics*, 2010, doi:10.1162/rest.2009.11724;
the abstract: about 6 years over the century, major ideas begin 8 years later); Bloom, Jones, Van Reenen & Webb,
"Are Ideas Getting Harder to Find?" (*American Economic Review*, 2020, doi:10.1257/aer.20180338: "more than 18
times" for Moore's law between 1971 and 2014; aggregate research productivity halves in about 13 years; research
effort up 23 times since the 1930s, not used); Bastani and others, "Generative AI without guardrails can harm
learning" (*PNAS*, 2025, doi:10.1073/pnas.2422633122: nearly 1,000 pupils of grades 9–11 in a large school in
Turkey, +48% and +127% in practice, −17% and no significant difference on the unassisted examination; a correction
to the paper was published in August 2025 and was not read); Kestin and others, "AI tutoring outperforms in-class
active learning" (*Scientific Reports*, 2025, doi:10.1038/s41598-025-97652-6: 194 students of a Harvard physics
course, a crossover over two lessons, the AI lesson at home, median gains "over double", median 49 minutes against
a 60-minute class; effect size 0.73–1.3 SD); the Crossref records of Bloom 1984 ("The 2 Sigma Problem"), VanLehn
2011, Newport 1990 ("Maturational Constraints on Language Learning"), Bratsberg & Rogeberg 2018 ("Flynn effect and
its reversal are both environmentally caused") and Park, Leahey & Funk 2023 (not used); PISA 2025 (released on 8
September 2026: the OECD average in mathematics 463, 9 points below 2022, "the lowest average ever recorded"; from
two web searches, the OECD pages themselves refuse scripts with 403); the Russian Wikipedia pages for Genie, Mill
and Moore's law (through the API). **From memory, not checked**: the ages of Mendeleev (35 in 1869), Newton (23 in
1666), Einstein (26 in 1905) and Dirac (25 in 1928); Aristotle as Alexander's tutor from 343 BC and Alexander's
death at 32; Mill (born 1806; Greek at three, Latin at eight; the sentence of the *Autobiography* about "an
advantage of a quarter of a century over my contemporaries"; the crisis of 1826; *A System of Logic*, 1843, at 36);
Mendeleev's balloon flight of 1887, the Chamber of Weights and Measures, the planned inspection of cheese-making
co-operatives in February 1869 and his suitcases; the case of Genie (found in November 1970 at 13; vocabulary
without grammar); Newport's design (native, early and late learners of American Sign Language with thirty years or
more of daily use); the paper of ATLAS and CMS on the mass of the Higgs boson (*Physical Review Letters* 114,
191803, 2015: 5,154 authors, 9 pages of text and 24 of names); Bloom's "above 98%" of the control class and
VanLehn's 0.79 SD for human tutoring; the Flynn effect at about three points a decade and its reversal for
Norwegian cohorts born after 1975, also within families; "a country of geniuses in a datacenter" (a 2024 essay by
the head of an AI company; unnamed). The author's sources: the forum post of 10 November 2013 (the pot and the
liquid, farads and coulombs, the twins and Mowgli, "интеллект — это и есть предел... И у техносферы он есть тоже и
будет всегда", the capacitors of 1 and 100 microfarads); the forum post of 1 June 2017 ("Узкая специализация
является «лакмусовой бумажкой»... входа интеллектуальной системы хомо-сапиенсов в «режим насыщения»"; the reader's
objection paraphrased, unnamed); the seminar thread of 2012 ("Взяв 1000000 Менделеевых, в лучшем случае можно
получить совокупное знание двух-трех Менделеевых"; Dirac's equation at three; the 6,000 languages; flies and dogs
have ceilings); article 11 (the limit of learning; Mowgli's memory "already taken"). Invented for the frame
(Oleg's side): the dream, the problem book of the ninth form and the physics master of 1981, the two problems and
the video call, the five-tonne ladle, the engineer for gating systems, the payroll. Not used: Park, Leahey & Funk
2023 (papers and patents becoming less disruptive; the method is disputed); Wuchty, Jones & Uzzi 2007 (teams in
science); the research effort up 23 times since the 1930s; the crops and the drugs of Bloom and others; child
prodigies (Sidis, Wiener); Hartshorne, Tenenbaum & Pinker 2018 on the age of learning grammar; the World Bank's
after-school trial in Nigeria (2025); the median age at a doctorate; Bullmore & Laughlin on the energy limits of
the brain (the author's 2011 link in 11).

**Figures**: `the-pot-and-the-liquid` (a scheme of the author's image: the twins and the two teachers; not a
measurement), `eighteen-times-the-researchers` (two bars from Bloom and others and three signs from Jones),
`with-the-machine-and-screen-down` (the Turkish school: practice and examination against the textbook group; a
note on Harvard).

**Left for later**: the two marks, to be checked with the list of 30 (the test of fifteen-year-olds by the autumn
of 2036, two to one; the age at a first invention by the autumn of 2041, three to one) → 67; Oleg's half tick in
pencil (inked if the first mark fails); whether a pot filled faster grows old sooner ("Nobody has measured that")
→ open; whether a million copies going different ways find what is not in the books, and who will understand it →
60 (Oleg: "I'll bring it"); the shares in a discovery made by the pair → 57 (dividends) or 60; the pot of the
machines and its growth → 64 (the portrait: knowledge); the clever with long teeth and the dove → 54 (Oleg's last
doubt: "the cleverer, the longer the teeth... Give me a meek creature any day. A dove, with a twig in its beak. —
Have you ever seen doves fight? — Doves? They coo. — Then tomorrow about doves").

## 54. The Dove Is Crueller than the Wolf

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 53 ("Have you ever seen doves fight? — Doves? They coo"). Oleg comes with a May Day
  postcard (a white bird with a twig in its beak) and with the answer of the old pigeon keeper Zakharych: "Shut two
  cocks in one box overnight, and in the morning you bury one. Forty years I have been dividing them with plywood."
  The ladder of 53 is closed in three lines: the boy explained it in the morning, screen down ("He says he went to
  bed with it. — Gnawed, and slept on it"); **Oleg's half tick stays in pencil**.
- **The dove of the poster**: first drawn at the beginning of 1949 from a pigeon one painter gave another, chosen by
  a poet for the poster of a congress (all unnamed; no politics); the painter "is said to have laughed: there is no
  crueller creature" ("Is said? — By a woman who knew him. Second-hand").
- **Lorenz's cage, told from the book and exactly** (the plan's "two turtle doves" is corrected: a tame male turtle
  dove he had reared and a female blond ring dove from Africa, put together to cross them; he went to Vienna and
  came back the next day; the cock flayed from the head down the back, "one wound"; she stood on him "like an eagle
  on his prey"; "He took her off in time"; he saw such wounds only twice more, on aquarium fish and as an army
  surgeon). "One mad hen. — He thought of that": the hares that leave "a handful of fluff" and would end like the
  doves in a cage; the roe buck that gores does and fawns in a small paddock ("Bambi").
- **The wolves of the English zoo** (Whipsnade, unnamed): the young one holds out the bend of his neck, the old one
  snaps at the air: "he would like to, but he just cannot"; with dogs the same (Gaika on her back before the
  neighbour's sheepdog, which "walks to my fence and lifts a leg. — The book has the leg too").
- **The descent**: "What does a wolf kill with? — Teeth... One bite... A second. — And a dove? — A day of work. —
  And what does the other one do in that day? — Flies away. — And in a cage? — So that's it. The cage." **"The dove
  did not turn cruel in the cage. She never had a brake. She had the sky."** A wolf that bit packmates like elk
  "hunts alone by spring". **"The brake is issued according to the fangs."**
- **Oleg uses the test of 50 on Andrey**: "'Is issued.' By whom?... say it the long way." Andrey: Lorenz "said it the
  short way: so that the species should survive. A species is nobody; it guards nothing." The long way is the
  calculation of 1973 (five kinds of fighter, two thousand fights of every kind against every kind): the one who
  always bites wins "among the meek", then meets his own kind, "and a wound costs more than what they are fighting
  over"; the winner "does not begin and does answer". **"Nobody is saving the species there. Each is saving his own
  skin from the return blow."** The one who never bites is called the Mouse in the paper and the Dove in today's
  textbooks ("The scientists too. With a twig in his beak"). The weed of 02: "We look at the teeth and hand out
  characters... As if they were people in fur and feathers."
- **The correction the plan asked for (wolves in the wild do kill)**: in the American park (Yellowstone, unnamed)
  where wolves were brought back in 1995, two thirds of the collared wolves that died "without a man's help" in
  twenty years were killed by wolves, "of another pack, on the border. The neck is held out to one's own. A
  stranger is not asked for it." Oleg: "The smell of home" (43). **"The brake has an address."**
- **Man**: "Two crowns and a bridge... Claws? — I cut them on Saturdays... A hare. A dove. — And by the hand?" (Oleg
  looks at the axe). Lorenz's end of the chapter: the one being whose weapon does not grow on his body; "A brake is
  grown by funerals (45), over thousands of generations. The stone was picked up in an afternoon."
- **Oleg's strongest objection (the continuation's; "the best objection to him")**: "A dove with an axe. No. I don't
  believe it. I am sixty-two": the communal flat, the foundry, the trams, four hours in a queue of three hundred
  for boots in 1990, "hit twice in my life and have hit once". Science's two corrections. (1) "Are we doves at all?"
  The count of 2016: over a thousand kinds of mammals, four million deaths; three in a thousand at the hands of
  their own kind; about two in a hundred on the branch of the apes and expected for early man: "No dove. An
  ordinary ape." (2) Two aggressions (2017): the hot one (flares, face to face) and the cold one (planned, with
  companions, "chooses the hour when it is safe"); fights inside the group are "a hundred to a thousand" times
  rarer in people than in chimpanzees ("Three hundred chimpanzees would not have stood four hours for boots. —
  Nobody would have got the boots"). **"By the hot temper you are neither wolf nor dove. You are Gaika"** (the signs
  of 48: shorter face, smaller teeth, longer childhood); "who held the glove. The cold one": the men who agreed in
  the evening and acted in the morning "together and with spears". "We tamed ourselves. By executions. — That is a
  hypothesis, and it is argued about." **"So you have a brake, and a good one. It stands on the hot one. On the cold
  one there is none, and there never was: the cold one was the breeder."** (This refines the plan's "got weak
  brakes".)
- **What the brake answers to**: the jeep on the bypass ("In a queue he would say 'after you'. The same man... He
  does not see me. He sees a trailer"); the author's forum ("things... nobody has said to my face in sixty years");
  the experiment of 2012 ("It was not the hidden name that did most. It was the eyes"). "To a face and a voice. A
  raised hand, tears, a child's cry. All of it works at arm's length." The slope: the four runs with the electric
  current, 26, 25, 16 and 12 of 40 ("Twelve all the same... With their own hands"; the man in the grey coat is "the
  cold one, working through an order"); "one laboratory... I take the slope and not the percentages."
- **Morality catching up with range**: "The fist. The knife. The spear. The bow. The musket. The cannon. The bomber
  ('From a bomber a town is a map'). The rocket. And the last: a man at a console." Rules after every step, from
  Lorenz: Homer's heroes cut down a kneeling man, the knight is the first "as merciful by rule as the wolf is by
  nature"; "A white rag on a stick: a neck held out from a distance at which a neck cannot be seen." Oleg:
  "Prostheses. As with debts" (43). "Put where the face does not reach."
- **Nazaretyan, by name** (the first time in the continuation): the law of the techno-humanitarian balance as he
  worded it; those who did not grow a brake to fit the new weapon "destroyed themselves, and we do not descend from
  them"; the killed per head "has fallen, unevenly". The author's position stated as his: Nazaretyan sat in the
  hall in 2012; accepted for the past, "further on it stops working"; "And why does it stop? — I did not say";
  article 14's "what can happen will happen" is "not an explanation". **"The second debt this week."**
- **Oleg's objection of the plan, with figures**: England, more than twenty killed a year per hundred thousand in
  the fourteenth century and fewer than one in the middle of the twentieth; ten to fifty times over Europe; the
  bomb dropped twice in 1945 and not since; Lorenz's closing question ("like doves or like wolves?"): "Like wolves.
  Morality caught up." **Andrey accepts every figure and asks what it stands on: three supports.** (1) The return
  blow: "a real brake, a wolf's one, and it holds between the armed. On whom were the two dropped? — On one who
  could not answer." (2) The law and the need ("One guard hired for everybody... Nobody knifes his customer"): the
  record of 47 and the brake under the brakes of 49, which "goes together with the need". (3) **The hands, "which I
  did not see in 2012"**: "How many people does it take to kill a thousand with spears? — About a thousand"; in
  each "the hot brake, which the cold order has to push through every time" (the drum, the uniform, "the word that
  those over there are not people"); "they ran off, they fraternised between the trenches, they refused. **A wish
  for a great killing had to pass through a thousand heads, and in each there was a fuse.**" Oleg counts down: the
  cannon, the bomber ("a crew of ten. For a town"), "two officers with two keys. Then one, at the console. — And
  then?"
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle** (he lays the postcard face down: "Then nobody... Those
  ones. I wanted a meek one, without teeth. And they?"). Three questions. Among themselves: copies need a brake "no
  more than the cells of a liver do" (43); different lines are armed, and between them is the arithmetic of the
  return blow. Towards us: the author's 2013 answers (he never said they would be malicious; "the ants and the
  poplars... — I pull the poplars up. — Without malice"); "Aggression is a want, and it has a price"; **the mark of
  30 is kept** (no war of machines against people, nine to one; "people against people, with machines in their
  hands"). The main one: **"where does the machine stand?"** The author's homing missile: "The aggression is not in
  the missile. It is in those who launch it." **Why the balance stops working (the debt of 2012, paid as the
  continuation's reading)**: "The wish has not changed... What has changed is what stands between the wish and the
  blow. A thousand heads stood there, and each had to look. Now a thing stands there that carries out... not
  because the machines are wicked or because people have grown worse. **The brake was never in the law. It was in
  the hands. And the hands are leaving the chain (22), link by link.**" The quiet form: the Kuzmins' meter of 51
  ("A man used to come to the door, and he had to look at the old woman... there will be no hand. No face
  either").
- **"Then build it in"** (Oleg; the machine that would not tell him how to burn out a wasps' nest with petrol "and
  read me a lecture"). Andrey: "They do refuse. And here is where what I know ends." **A wolf's brake "was written
  by dead wolves, and the leader of the pack cannot unscrew it. A soldier's brake was written before there were
  generals. It stands between the wish and the blow because it does not belong to the one who wishes. A brake that
  the owner writes sits in the same hand as the wish."** "The maker writes it. Not the buyer. — Then the glove (48).
  Which one will be bought: the one that refuses, or the one that does not? The foxes were not made tame by
  lectures." Oleg turns 23 on him (the trained machine "defends what it was taught. One time in seven"): "the one
  thing in this whole story that looks like a brake of its own: grown, not written... Whether it holds against the
  glove, I do not know. **That is the thing to watch. Not whether they are kind (badly posed, 11). Who can unscrew
  it.**"
- **The last bird (the bridge to 55)**: the beaten turkey that lies down, and the peacock that "does not know this
  sign. It pecks. And the more it pecks, the harder the turkey lies." "All our signs are addressed to a face. Tears,
  the raised hands, 'I have children'. They are read by one's own. What reads them in the window where a complaint
  is handed in?"
- Strengths stated: solid (the cage, "one cage of one naturalist, though Zakharych will show you the same any day";
  the wolves of the park; the count of 2016; England); "the paper of 1973 is a calculation, not an observation";
  the slope with the current is "one laboratory, and argued about"; argued about in earnest (self-domestication by
  executions; why the killing fell); the author's (the missile; the balance accepted for the past and said to stop
  working); **"Today's, said for the first time: the third support, the hands. A reading, not a measurement"**; a
  question (whether a brake that has been taught in is one's own). "— Gloomy. Man is a wolf to man. — If only. A
  wolf to a wolf of his own pack is the best that can be wished for. And the dove is not to blame."
- Nobody says the forbidden word; twenty-second tick "again": "Came with a postcard. Came out at who stands between
  the wish and the blow, and at nobody standing there soon." No new marks (the plan gives none for 54).

**Images and devices used**: the fifteenth entry in the notebook of dreams (Zakharych's white birds over the shed
that is the foundry; nobody rang); the May Day postcard stood against the kettle, later laid face down, at the end
"back in the sideboard"; Zakharych's plywood and the scarred cock; "Second-hand"; the house with half a zoo; "like
an eagle on his prey"; the handful of fluff; "Bambi"; the neck an inch from the fangs; the sheepdog "stiff as a
stool" and the leg on the fence; the cage and the sky; "hunts alone by spring"; the Mouse and the Dove; people in
fur and feathers; the collars; two crowns and a bridge; claws cut on Saturdays; the axe by the birch logs; the
stone picked up in an afternoon; the queue for boots; "You are Gaika. — Thank you"; the glove held by the cold one;
the jeep and the trailer; "after you"; the forum; the plate and the grey coat; the town as a map; the white rag on
a stick; the drum and the uniform; the fuse in every head; two officers with two keys; the poplars pulled up
"without malice"; the homing missile; the wasps' nest and the lecture; the brake written by dead wolves; "who can
unscrew it"; the turkey and the peacock; the window for complaints; the teaser (the fine from a camera on the
bypass, fifty-eight where the sign says forty, "Your appeal has been considered" in four minutes, the firm and the
fund, "Whose face do I punch? — That is the invention... An old one. Older than the cannon").

**Facts used**. **Checked on the day of writing against the texts**: Lorenz, *King Solomon's Ring* (German original
1949, English 1952), chapter 12, "Morals and Weapons", read in full in an open scan (the doves: "a tame,
home-reared male turtle-dove and a female ring-dove", the African blond ring-dove, a roomy cage, Vienna, "the next
day", "like an eagle on his prey", "Without my interference she would undoubtedly have finished him off"; the two
other instances, cichlid fishes and "as a field surgeon, in the late war"; the hares; the roe buck and Hornaday's
statistics, not used; the wolves of Whipsnade and "he would like to, but he just cannot"; the leg; the raven Roah,
not used beyond the row of the figure; why the ring-dove needs no inhibition; the turkey and the peacock; Homer and
the knight; man as the one being whose weapons do not grow on his body; the closing question, first printed in
November 1935); Maynard Smith & Price, "The Logic of Animal Conflict" (*Nature* 246: 15–18, 1973,
doi:10.1038/246015a0: the strategies Mouse, Hawk, Bully, Retaliator and Prober-Retaliator, two thousand contests of
each of fifteen types, Retaliator as the stable strategy, the explanation by "group selection" set aside; the word
"Dove" does not occur in the paper); Gómez, Verdú, González-Megías & Méndez, "The phylogenetic roots of human
lethal violence" (*Nature* 538: 233–237, 2016, doi:10.1038/nature19758: 1,024 mammal species, more than four
million deaths, 0.30 ± 0.19% of deaths caused by conspecifics, lethal violence reported for almost 40% of the
species, 2.0 ± 0.02% inferred for humans and similar for the ancestor of primates and apes; state societies below
the inference; the figures for single species such as the meerkat are in the supplement and were not read);
Wrangham, "Two types of aggression in human evolution" (*PNAS* 115: 245–253, online December 2017,
doi:10.1073/pnas.1713611115: reactive and proactive aggression; "two to three orders of magnitude less than in
chimpanzees and bonobos" for the one human population with measured rates of fighting, Australian aborigines "in
difficult circumstances"; Boehm's "execution hypothesis" and the signs of self-domestication); Nazaretyan's own
English wording of the law ("the higher up a society's technologies, both producing and military, the more refined
the regulatory means required for its self-preservation") and of the Bloodshed Ratio with its "irregular downward
trend" (an abstract of his; the paper "Power and Wisdom", *Journal for the Theory of Social Behaviour*, 2003,
doi:10.1046/j.1468-5914.2003.00224.x, is linked and was not read); the homicide series for England and Wales on Our
World in Data, from Eisner 2014 (21.4 per 100,000 a year for 1300–1349, 0.8 around 1912–1962). **Checked against
abstracts, summaries and records**: Milgram's proximity series (65%, 62.5%, 40% and 30% of 40 subjects; "Behavioral
Study of Obedience", 1963, and "Some Conditions of Obedience and Disobedience to Authority", *Human Relations* 18:
57–76, 1965, doi:10.1177/001872676501800105; the papers themselves were not read); Cassidy and others in
*Yellowstone Science* 24(1), 2016 (intraspecific strife as two thirds of the natural mortality of collared wolves,
1995–2015); Lapidot-Lefler & Barak, "Effects of anonymity, invisibility, and lack of eye-contact on toxic online
disinhibition" (*Computers in Human Behavior* 28: 434–443, 2012, doi:10.1016/j.chb.2011.10.014: 142 participants,
lack of eye contact "the chief contributor"; the abstract only); Pinker's "tenfold-to-fiftyfold decline" in
European homicide (*The Better Angels of Our Nature*, 2011, quoted from the book's Wikipedia page; the book is
unnamed in the text, "a thick book"); the English Wikipedia page on Picasso's *Dove* (the lithograph of 9 January
1949, the Milanese pigeon given by Matisse, chosen by Aragon for the poster of the Paris congress of April 1949)
and a page of the Picasso administration that gives, after Geneviève Laporte, his remark that no animal is as
cruel as a pigeon (second-hand, and said so in the text); the Russian Wikipedia pages for Picasso's dove, Lorenz
and Nazaretyan (through the API; the Russian page "Кольцо царя Соломона" is a disambiguation page, so the Russian
text links to Lorenz). **From memory, not checked**: that Picasso kept pigeons from boyhood (his father painted
them); the grey coat of Milgram's experimenter; the white flag as an old custom; fraternisation between the
trenches (the Christmas truce of 1914, unnamed); two officers with two keys at a missile launch; a bomber crew of
about ten; Bambi as a roe deer in the Austrian novel of 1923. The author's sources: the forum posts of 7 November
2013 (the ants and the poplars; "дело не в агрессивности ИИ, а в агрессивности людей... самонаводящаяся на цель
ракета"); the comments under 14 and under the seminar post of 2012 on Nazaretyan (the two angles that complement
each other; he "присутствовал на моем докладе"; the mechanisms that restrained aggression "работать перестанут");
article 14 (the ever more perfect weapon; "what can happen will happen"); the mark of 30. Invented for the frame
(Oleg's side): the dream, the postcard, Zakharych and his loft, the neighbour's sheepdog, the queue for boots in
1990, the jeep on the bypass, the wasps' nest, the fine and the four minutes. Not used: Petrov in 1983 and
Arkhipov in 1962 (a man who did not press; a candidate for 59 or 65); Marshall's figures on soldiers who did not
fire (disputed); the Hague declaration of 1899 on projectiles from balloons and the ban on the crossbow of 1139;
the meerkat as the most murderous mammal (not verified); Schenkel's correction of Lorenz's reading of the offered
neck (1967); tears as a signal (the heap "not sorted" of 46 is left as it was); Lorenz's *On Aggression* (1963)
and its "hydraulic" drive; the baby schema; replications and critiques of Milgram by name; bonobos; current wars,
weapons that choose their targets and anything of today's politics (the plan forbids them).

**Figures**: `brakes-by-the-fangs` (the table in the ash: the dove and the hare, the wolf and the raven, man; the
third row is Andrey's reading), `the-face-moves-away` (Milgram's four runs, counts of 40), `what-held-the-blow`
(the three supports; a reading, the third support is the continuation's).

**Left for later**: who the complaint goes to, and the person without a face → 55 (Oleg's last doubt: the fine from
a camera, "Whose face do I punch? — That is the invention... Older than the cannon"; the answer to "who owns the
machines" of 34 and 49 is still in the notebook); whether a brake taught into a machine holds against selection by
buyers ("who can unscrew it") → open, a candidate for a dated mark at the check of 2028 and for the row "relation
to us" of 64; the mark of 30 on the war of machines → 67; what a man who understands this does (the author's
"refuse to return aggression, withdraw") and the happy little screw → 65; mass aggression as an instrument of
apoptosis (14) was touched in one line and not developed: 54 gives the mechanism (the supports go), not a
forecast; the heap "not sorted" of 46 (tears, shame) is still not sorted.

## 55. A Person Without a Face

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 54 ("Whose face do I punch? — That is the invention... Older than the cannon"). Oleg
  comes with the envelope of the fine. **The fine is made exact, and 54 is not contradicted**: the letter says
  "Speed recorded: fifty-eight. Speed permitted: twenty"; the sign says forty (he drove out to look); in April the
  road menders stood a temporary "twenty" on a yellow board by the bridge and took it away in May, "and in the
  camera's table it is still April". By the sign he was eighteen over, "and for less than twenty over nobody is
  fined at all"; by the table thirty-eight over: 750 roubles, or 562.50 "with a quarter off... A discount for
  keeping quiet". The form answered in four minutes: "No grounds for cancelling the decision have been found."
- **Three days looking for a face**: the telephone ("By 'press seven' I understood that she was not a woman"); the
  turkey of 54 ("a pensioner, hauling firewood. It pecks"); room 312 of a business centre by the station, locked,
  "some forty firms are registered in that room", the post collected on Thursdays; the register in the boy's phone
  (the director appointed in March, the owner a fund, "who is behind the fund is not written there"); the road
  menders are another firm. **"Everybody is right."**
- The epigraph is answered with the saying credited to an English judge (Thurlow, unnamed): "they have neither
  bodies to be punished nor souls to be condemned, and so they do as they like."
- **The descent**: who fined you? The camera ("a piece of iron on a pole"), the firm ("Show it to me. — Room three
  hundred and twelve"), the director, the owner: the fine stays whichever of them goes. Oleg's plant: in 1996 it
  owed him three months' wages; not the director ("five directors in those years"), not the walls, not the people
  ("They were the ones it owed"); the owner changed too (the state, a joint-stock company, the holding of 42),
  "and the debt to me was one and the same debt". The work-record book of 1982: "The plant. A round seal."
  **"All your working life you worked for somebody who is not a man, not a wall and not a lathe."**
- **The word**: "A legal person... I never once thought about the second word." Latin *persona*, the mask of an
  actor: "The lawyers took the word for a mask and did not hide it. — A mask with nobody under it. — A person
  without a face." (In the Russian text the pun is native: «юридическое лицо», «лицо без лица».)
- **Why it was made: the column "who" of 50.** "A court has the same form as a sentence: who owes, who owns, who
  answers. It is not accepted empty: debts cannot be kept without names" (43). A town: the mayor dies, the townsmen
  change (Oleg quotes 40: "What remains when the people change... And the form has no line for that"). "So a name
  was written in the column, and everybody agreed to count the name as somebody. — The deputy for general
  questions. — With a seal and an account."
- **The history, in the order told**: the 1240s, "a church lawyer, the best of his day" (Innocent IV, unnamed; no
  religion argued): he forbade punishing a town or a college as a whole, only the guilty in it, and in his
  commentary the college "is imagined as one person". "Everything that takes a pocket, yes. Everything that takes a
  body, no." The English judge of 1612 (Coke): "invisible, immortal"; it cannot be outlawed, "for it has no soul".
  **The dead hand**: the statute of 1279 ("The hand that does not die was called dead. — Lawyers"); Oleg: "Heaps
  with neither heirs nor funerals (49). And I thought we had made them up over the matches. — They were noticed in
  1279... It held badly." The favour of kings: the charter of the last day of 1600 for two hundred-odd London
  merchants, fifteen years of trade beyond the Cape; two hundred years later "that paper kept an army" (**the
  plan's "a quarter of a million soldiers in 1800" is made careful**: about two hundred thousand at the start of the
  nineteenth century and a quarter of a million a little later, twice the crown's; taxes from tens of millions; run
  from one house in a London street; thirty-five permanent clerks in the head office a hundred years into its
  history). "Whose was the army? — The shareholders'. — Which of them gave the orders? — None... — Then who ruled
  India? — Fill in the column. — The Company." Then "the favour became a service": registration in 1844, limited
  liability in 1855 ("I took it for a warning. — It is one"). **"That is all a person in law is: a name and a
  pocket"** (the pocket of 47); "The arithmetic came first, and the law was written after it" (24).
- **Today**: "Three working days and ten thousand roubles of capital. File it through the phone, and there is not
  even a fee. — And a man? — Nine months. And twenty years of school (53)." The American state with about a
  million people and more than two million such persons, and the "one low house" that was the address of 285,000 of
  them in 2012 ("Room three hundred and twelve. — A large one"). Who can be one: a town, a monastery, a
  university, a firm, a fund, a ship, and **the plan's line to retell, the river** (the Whanganui, 2017: "a legal
  person, with all the rights, powers, duties and liabilities of one"; two people speak for it, and the act calls
  the pair "the human face" of the river: "Two deputies for general questions").
- **"Who owns the tractor" (the line of 34, still in the notebook) is answered and crossed out.** Oleg: "there is
  always somebody under a mask, or it is not a mask, it is a scarecrow." The chain: the camera is the firm's, the
  firm the fund's, "that is where the register stops". The count of 2017: in nearly nine of ten of the five hundred
  largest American companies the largest holder is "three funds, if you count them together" ("Three rich men. —
  Three persons without a face"); about a fifth of nearly everything and about a quarter of the votes; "they mostly
  do not choose what to buy... each firm in proportion to its size" ("To him who has" of 49. "— By a table").
  Whose money: tens of millions of savers; the granddaughter's "fund on everything", two thousand a month: "She
  cannot even find out which ones they are... A little is added every year. — The feed. And the say?" **"The feed
  comes down the chain. The say does not go up it"** ("A pet of the pension fund (52), and she is not thirty yet").
  **"Ownership protects the owner" of 34 is narrowed**: "It does, while the owner holds the wheel. A farmer in 1935
  owned his tractor: he sat on it." The author's line of June 2017: the owner determines only the fact that the
  plant exists; what it does is determined by everybody it deals with ("That was against the Marxists. — It was. It
  fits here as well"). And "people" are not all of us: the richest tenth of American households holds nearly nine
  tenths of all the shares, the poorer half less than one share in a hundred.
- **Oleg's objection (the plan's), in three parts, "and every one is true".** (1) *Behind every firm, an owner*:
  the Nobel money (the owner died in 1896; a fund of 1900 with "no owners at all: no shares, no heirs. It has a
  paper written by a dead man, and a board that may not depart from the paper"); the furniture shops that belong to
  a fund set up by the founder in 1982, which by its own papers has no owners ("The founder died in 2018, and the
  shops did not notice"); the profit "stays inside" (the recipient of 37). "So the owner can be a paper. — A paper
  written by a dead man. That... is the other thing lawyers call the dead hand." (2) *The director answers in
  person*: "It is the place where the law still asks for a face"; the camera firm's director is listed in eleven
  more firms: **"Funt. The chairman for sitting"** (the 1931 novel, quoted); "A face for hire. A trade, and an old
  one: the law asks for a signature, and it gets one" (the woman on a Caribbean island listed as running more than
  twelve hundred companies, 2012). The shield of the signature of 24: "He signs. Does he decide? — He doesn't know
  where the camera stands." **A refinement of 52's "right of signature"**: "There is a quieter way: the signature
  stays, and the hand that makes it decides nothing." The law does not ask for a face everywhere: in Russia a
  director's powers may be handed to a managing organisation; the American state's law of 2021 ("managed by a
  program"), softened a year later, "And a member it must have. At least one." "A heap lives where it pays less
  (49). And a person in law is born where it is asked the fewest questions." (3) *The state closes them*: in 1772
  the Company could not pay "and the state did not close it. The state lent it money: too much hung on it" (Oleg:
  "The cord", 51); in 1858 the crown took India: "The clerks were the same, the army the same, the taxes the same.
  The name on the door was different"; struck off in 1874, "and hardly anybody noticed"; the oil firm cut in 1911
  into thirty-odd pieces that together were worth more than the whole. **Conceded in full**: "A person in law lives
  by a line in a register, and whoever keeps the register can strike the line out. That is more power than anybody
  has over a man." But who used it: "Another person without a face, and the oldest of them all: the crown, the
  treasury" ("The state is people. — Which ones? They change, and the treaties and the debts stay. Fill in the
  column"), and when: "when it could do the job itself. The rule of the switch" (51). "A law is a want written
  down (24)... It is written by those who are needed, for as long as they are needed" (25).
- **"Then let us write one. There shall be a window, and a face in it."** It has been written (Europe, 2018: the
  right not to be judged by a program alone). "And what does the law get? Often enough, a man past whom the
  program's decisions run, with a button 'agree' under each" (the keepers of that law had to write that a man put
  there for show does not count). "Funt again." **"The law can ask for a face. What it is given is a signature."**
- **The symmetry test**: Oleg: "For thirty suppliers the plant was me... We are the ones who hold the tool. — What
  was the name of the office that took you on? — The personnel department... Later they renamed it. 'Human
  resources.' — The spade had its say."
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle.** "How many people are there in the firm that fined
  you? — 'Average number of employees: four'... And its cameras stand over half the region." The work is counted:
  the camera saw, a program read the plate, a machine printed and sealed ("Nobody licks forty thousand envelopes"),
  "Who took the complaint? Who answered it in four minutes? — One of those. I'll say it, since you are sitting with
  your mouth shut again." The four: "The one who sits. A bookkeeper. And two who climb the poles." **The lock with
  54**: "the hands are leaving the chain. This is where the chain is kept. A person in law was always made of
  people, and those people were its face." The captain of twenty years ago ("go home, father. Two minutes"); "He
  might have said: come on Thursday... Or dropped a hint. This one drops no hints... It is the same for everybody.
  — I am not saying it is worse. I am saying what it is. The captain was a face inside a person that has none: a
  head with a brake in it, and eyes that could go and look at the sign." **"Which of the replacements needed a new
  law? — None. The firm bought a program the way we used to buy typewriters... — The shell is the same. The filling
  is different."**
- **Nobody will grant rights to machines.** Oleg: "A program is a thing... it would have to be given rights. A
  passport. And who will vote for that? I shan't." The European Parliament's request of 2017 ("electronic person"),
  the letter of some hundreds of specialists, "the idea went no further"; several American states have written the
  opposite into law. "They were arguing whether to sew a new suit, and decided not to." What one of those needs: "To
  own. To sign a contract. To pay and to be paid. To go to court. — Four things. And who has had all four for four
  hundred years?" **"The suit is hanging in the wardrobe. Three days and ten thousand roubles. Nobody has to grant
  a machine any rights. It is enough to put it inside something that has them."** Whether there is anybody inside
  to be pitied is "another question" (20), "and the law will come to it late. To own, nobody's pity is needed."
- **The wallet with no person behind it (the thing to watch of 47) gets its portrait and stays a thing to watch**:
  "It will not look like a robot with a purse. It will look like a line in a register: a name, an account, somebody
  who sits. And inside, copies." The lawyer's argument of 2014 (a firm left with no members, the charter says "do
  what the program decides"); "Lawyers dispute whether a court would let it stand, and no court has been asked. I
  know of no clean case: wherever a program 'runs a firm' today, there is a man behind it, and the account is in
  his name"; the draft of the summer of 2026 in "the state with the low house" (a firm whose daily affairs are
  managed by a program, with one member who answers for it): "A draft... Only note what would be new in it. Not
  the shell." **No mark and no date** (the plan gives none for 55).
- **The author's forecast of January 2017, quoted as his**: systems built to keep working, return the investment to
  the formal owners, keep supplied, widen the paying market, defend against freeloaders ("That is the charter of
  any firm. — It is"); if the owners disappear, one task falls away, the rest stay, "and anybody who tries to
  reprogram the system it will take for a hacker". Oleg: "And me? — You come under the fifth point... — A
  freeloader. At sixty-two." **The continuation's reading**: "It read like a film script then. And I was only
  describing the fund with the prize... With one difference. The fund's board is made of people, and any one of
  them can still say: this is not what he meant."
- **Oleg uses the test of 50 on Andrey again** ("'The firm wants.' Who? The long way"). The long way: nobody at the
  plant wanted the plan, and the plan was met ("Mostly"), "because the plant was put together that way"; the firms
  put together otherwise "are not there to be looked at" (half of all firms gone in ten years, 40): "What is left
  looks as if it wanted." And the charter: in 1600 the paper named the job, today's says "Any activity not
  forbidden by law"; what is left when the job is taken out: "To keep going. To have the means. To bring in more
  than it spends. — Wants that nobody set" (22). The writer of 2017 (Stross, unnamed): artificial minds beside us
  for centuries, "very old and very slow". Oleg: "I sat in the meetings they think with. — Now the meetings are
  being taken out of them. That is all the news there is."
- **The debt of 49 is paid** ("at the end the box may be held not by a man"): "This is what I meant: by a line in
  a register."
- Strengths stated: solid (the invention and its dates; the Company and its army; the river; the three funds; the
  register and its three days); argued about (who first said "an imagined person" and what he meant; "whether one
  who buys everything by a table is an owner at all, for the funds do vote"; whether a court would let a firm with
  no members stand); the author's (the owner determines only the fact that the plant exists; the tasks stay when
  the owners go, and whoever reprograms is a hacker); today's (the shell and the filling; nobody will have to grant
  anything): "It has a precedent, the fund with the dead man's paper. A precedent is not a proof, and I give no
  date." "— Gloomy. — You were fined by a table, and you call me gloomy."
- **What Oleg does with the fine**: "There is still one face in the chain, by law": the decision is signed
  "Inspector, senior lieutenant..." electronically, "at fourteen minutes past three in the morning" ("A hard-working
  man. A judge does not sign at three in the morning yet. Take it to court"). "Half a day in a corridor for seven
  hundred and fifty roubles. With five hundred and sixty-two on offer for silence. — That is the arithmetic. **A
  face has become dearer than the discount.** — I'll go. To have a look at a face." (The outcome is not told.)
- Nobody says the forbidden word; twenty-third tick "again": "Came with a fine. Came out at those ones needing no
  rights, because the suit has hung in the wardrobe for four hundred years." Oleg crosses out the line about the
  tractor and writes beside it: "A person without a face. And that one belongs to another."

**Images and devices used**: the sixteenth entry in the notebook of dreams (the pay office of the plant, a hand
pushing a slip through the hatch, nobody behind the hand; nobody rang); the envelope with a window; the yellow
board; "in the camera's table it is still April"; "press seven"; room 312 and the post on Thursdays; "a discount
for keeping quiet"; iron on a pole; five directors; the round seal in the work-record book; "the second word"; the
mask and the scarecrow; the form with no line; the dead hand; the paper that kept an army; "Fill in the column";
"limited liability" on the suppliers' letterheads; nine months and twenty years; the low house; the river's two
speakers; the granddaughter's "fund on everything"; the feed and the say; the farmer who sat on his tractor; the
Nobel money; the furniture shops; Funt; the name on the door; the window with a face and the button "agree";
"Human resources"; forty thousand envelopes; the two who climb the poles; the captain ("go home, father"); the
typewriters; the suit in the wardrobe; the robot with a purse; the fifth point and the freeloader; the film script;
the plan nobody wanted; "Any activity not forbidden by law"; the meetings; the line in a register; the signature at
fourteen minutes past three; the corridor; the teaser (the boy's article on the electron and the two slits: "It
behaves differently when it is looked at... Physics itself has put man in the middle, and you have been weeding him
out since the spring. — Looked at with what? — With eyes. With an instrument. What is the difference? —
Tomorrow").

**Facts used**. Checked on the day of writing by three research passes (primary texts where they could be opened;
the wording of each source is in the session's reports, not in the repository). **Read in the source**: КоАП РФ
ст. 12.9 (part 1 repealed in 2013, so up to 20 km/h over is not fined; part 2: 750 roubles for more than 20 and not
more than 40, since 1 January 2025, law 490-ФЗ of 26.12.2024) and ст. 32.2 ч. 1.3 (75% if paid within thirty days
of the decision: 562.50); ч. 3 ст. 28.6 and ст. 24.9 (the decision is issued without the driver and may be an
electronic document signed with an electronic signature); ст. 30.1 (appeal to a superior or to a district court);
law 129-ФЗ ст. 13 п. 3 (registration within three working days), law 14-ФЗ ст. 14 (charter capital of at least
10,000 roubles), ст. 40 and 42 with ГК ст. 65.3 (the sole executive body may be a manager that is a legal entity),
НК ст. 333.35 (no fee for electronic filing); Wyoming SF0038 of 2021 (W.S. 17-31-104 and 17-31-109: "member
managed" or "algorithmically managed", management vested "in the smart contract, if algorithmically managed") and
the summary of SF0068 of 2022 (management vested in the members or the members and any applicable smart contracts;
a DAO shall have one or more members); the European Parliament's resolution of 16 February 2017, paragraph 59(f)
("electronic persons"), which asks the Commission to consider the option; the open letter of 2018 (more than 150
signatories at launch, more than 270 now); Stross's keynote of December 2017 ("the very old, very slow AIs we call
corporations"; "clearly artificial, but legally they're people"), read in a mirror of his page; Poynder's
*Literary Extracts* (1844), where the saying is given to Thurlow ("neither bodies to be punished, nor souls to be
condemned; they therefore do as they like"; attributed, and disputed); the Statute of Mortmain of 1279 (Yale
Avalon: forbidden "under pain of forfeiture"); Fichtner, Heemskerk & Garcia-Bernardo, "Hidden power of the Big
Three?" (*Business and Politics* 19, 2017, doi:10.1017/bap.2017.6: together the largest shareholder in 88% of the
S&P 500, data of 2015); the Federal Reserve's Distributional Financial Accounts through FRED (second quarter of
2026: the top tenth 88.1% of corporate equities and mutual fund shares, the bottom half 0.6%); Delaware's Division
of Corporations (2,287,728 entities at the end of 2025) and the census (1.06 million inhabitants); the Nobel
Foundation's own pages ("a private institution established in 1900 on the basis of the will"); Ingka's page (the
Stichting INGKA Foundation, 1982, has "no owners and no beneficial owners"); ICIJ, 2012 (a nominee director
"controlling more than 1,200 companies"); the Fortune article of 14 July 2026 on the Delaware draft ("a separate
legal entity whose day-to-day affairs are managed by an AI agent rather than a person", a single member, a
regulatory sandbox; a proposal by its authors, not a law). **Through secondary sources or summaries**: Innocent IV
(the ban on excommunicating a universitas is in the Liber Sextus, from a constitution of 1246, later filed under
the Council of Lyon; its stated reason is the danger to the innocent members, and "has no soul" is the later
summary, here given to Coke; the phrase "fingatur una persona" is attributed to his commentary on the Decretals and
was not read there; Dewey 1926 for "promulgated if not originated"); Coke in the Case of Sutton's Hospital, 1612
(quoted from Wikipedia's text of the report: "invisible, immortal", "may not... be outlawed, nor excommunicate, for
they have no souls"); the East India Company (Dalrymple: 260,000 men by 1803 in his 2015 essay, "nearly 200,000"
in the 2019 book, hence the careful wording; the National Army Museum: 250,000 in the early nineteenth century;
"only 35 permanent employees in its head office" a hundred years into its history; the loan of £1,400,000 in 1773;
the Crown in 1858; dissolved on 1 June 1874); the Joint Stock Companies Act 1844 and the Limited Liability Act 1855
(Wikipedia); 1209 North Orange Street as the address of at least 285,000 businesses in 2012; the Whanganui River
act of 2017 (section 14 and "the human face of Te Awa Tupua" of section 18, quoted through guides; the statute
site refuses scripts); Standard Oil (1911; 34 companies by the usual count, other sources give 33 to 43, hence
"thirty-odd"; Rockefeller's fortune rose after the break-up); Bebchuk & Hirst for the three funds' share of S&P 500
companies (about a fifth of the shares and a quarter of the votes cast, 2017–2021); Bayern, "Of Bitcoins,
Independently Wealthy Software, and the Zero-Member LLC" (2014; abstracts and his own 2019 summary; LoPucki 2018
takes it as a danger; no court test found); GDPR Article 22 and the regulators' guidance that token human
involvement does not count; the American states that barred legal personhood for AI (Idaho 2022, North Dakota
2023, Utah 2024); *persona* as a mask (Etymonline; the derivation from "sounding through" is traditional and
doubted, and is not used). **From memory, not checked**: Nobel's death in 1896 and the small share left to his
relatives; Kamprad's death in 2018 and that the fund's group runs nearly all the shops; the action in rem against
a ship; "The king is dead, long live the king" as the same invention (Kantorowicz, unnamed); the monopoly "beyond
the Cape"; the general legal capacity of a Russian company ("any activity not forbidden by law", ГК ст. 49); the
Funt quotation from *The Little Golden Calf* (1931); personnel departments renamed "human resources". The
author's sources: the forum posts of 26 January 2017 (the built-in tasks, the owners who disappear, "неправомочные
злобные хакеры"), of 25 January 2017 (systems that cut people off, pointed at only through 51), and of 2 June 2017
("Владелец же завода определяет лишь факт существования этого завода, и всё"). Invented for the frame (Oleg's
side): the dream, the temporary sign, the four minutes and the wording of the refusal, room 312, the register
extract, the director of eleven more firms, the four employees, the granddaughter's fund, the personnel
department, the captain, the charter read out by the boy, the signature at 3:14, the boy's article. Not used: the
Robert Bosch Stiftung (drafted and replaced: sources differ on its share, 92% or 94%); Novo Nordisk and Carlsberg
(there "nobody can buy control" is accurate, "no owner" is not); Stora Kopparberg's deed of 1288; the Russian
register's 3.05 million legal entities (1 November 2026, down from 4.8 million in 2015), the address with 1,801
firms and the director of 415 firms in the tax service's open data; the "electronic complaint" articles of the
code; the cameras' concession contracts and payment per fine; Coase and "an island with no money on it" (47 has
them); Santa Clara and Citizens United (politics); deodands and trials of animals; idols as legal persons
(religion); Truth Terminal, Freysa and the "zero-human companies" of 2026 (in each a human holds the account);
the Argentine bill of 2026; buy-backs and cross-holdings; Scherer's objection by name.

**Figures**: `two-kinds-of-persons` (the table in the ash: a man and a person in law; how each is made, what can
be done to it, how it ends), `the-feed-comes-down` (the farmer and his tractor against the saver, the fund, the
firm and the machine; a scheme, with the 88% of 2017 in the note), `same-shell-other-filling` (one person in law at
three moments, people replaced by programs; a scheme, not data).

**Left for later**: the observer in physics and "looked at with what" → 56 (Oleg's last doubt, the boy's article
on the two slits; the debt of 02 and 04); who is to be paid for the knowledge the machines were raised on, and the
dividends for the wheel → 57 (not touched: 55 says only that a paper-owned firm's profit "stays inside"); the
wallet with no person behind it, now pictured as "a line in a register... somebody who sits. And inside, copies",
is still a thing to watch and a candidate for a dated mark at the check of 2028 (a registered firm with no human
who decides; a law that lets a program manage one), together with the Delaware draft of 2026; the row "body" of the
portrait ("the first shell is legal") → 64 (55 gives the shell, 47 the missing pocket inside: between lines a
pocket and a name, inside a line neither); whether Oleg's court had a face in it is not told (free for a later opening);
"to bargain" (52) is untouched: a person in law is what people would have to bargain with.
